12	**Covers:** SNAKE-01–SNAKE-09, FISH-01–FISH-10, HUNT-01–HUNT-09, CORE-01–CORE-07, AGENT-01–AGENT-06, PG-01–PG-05, SELF-01–SELF-04, BASE-06–BASE-08, ABL-01–ABL-03, RUN-01–RUN-04, DATA-02, QR-01–QR-03, QR-05–QR-07
13	
14	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
15	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
16	| 1 | `load_settings` | core.settings | path → RunSettings | TOML into the frozen dataclasses; unknown names raise `ValueError` naming the key | RUN-01 |
17	| 2 | `make_env` | lab.build | RunSettings → Env | `if` on `settings.env`: Snake, Corridor, FishTank (with `n_agents` fish) or PredatorPrey, returned as a zero-argument factory | CORE-01, DATA-02 |
18	| 3 | `Snake` | envs.snake | view, Rewards → Env | full rules on 10×10, compact and grid views | SNAKE-01–SNAKE-09 |
19	| 4 | `Corridor` | envs.corridor | length, Rewards → Env | 10 cells, one-hot position, 50-step cap | CORE-03 |
20	| 5 | `FishTank` | envs.tank | view, n_fish, Rewards → Env | vectorised ball reflection, shortened swim vectors, wall stop, catches, 1,000-step cap, full view (AD-12) | FISH-01–FISH-09 |
21	| 6 | `PredatorPrey` | envs.predprey | Rewards → Env | the tank's movement functions (AD-16) plus pellets, catches, 50-step prey respawns, both teams' limited views, 1,000-step cap | HUNT-01–HUNT-09 |
22	| 7 | `make_agent` | lab.build | RunSettings, Env → Agent | one agent per team of `env.teams`: learners from `settings.agent` (tabular, dqn, reinforce or ppo) sized from the team's `TeamSpec`, other teams scripted or random per `settings.opponents`; ablation switches from `settings.ablation` | ABL-01–ABL-03, PG-05, HUNT-07, SELF-02 |
23	| 8 | `TabularAgent` | agents.tabular | AgentParams, Exploration → Agent | dict of NumPy rows keyed on observation bytes; linear exploration decay | AGENT-01, AGENT-02, AGENT-06, ABL-03 |
24	| 9 | `DQNAgent` | agents.dqn | AgentParams, Exploration → Agent | 128×128 MLP, uniform memory, hard target refresh, Huber loss, gradient clip 10 | AGENT-02–AGENT-06, ABL-01, ABL-02 |
25	| 10 | `ReinforceAgent` | agents.reinforce | ActionSpace, AgentParams → Agent | Gaussian 64×64 Tanh policy with a learned log-std; one update per `games_per_update` games on normalised returns-to-go (AD-11) | PG-01, PG-02, AGENT-06 |
26	| 11 | `PPOAgent` | agents.ppo | ActionSpace, AgentParams → Agent | the same policy plus a 64×64 value net; GAE, clipped objective, `epochs` passes over minibatches, one instance for every fish (AD-11) | PG-01, PG-03, PG-04, AGENT-06, SELF-01 |
27	| 12 | `ScriptedPredator` | baselines.scripted_predator | () → Agent | length-1 vector toward the nearest visible prey, else toward the centre | BASE-06 |
28	| 13 | `ScriptedPrey` | baselines.scripted_prey | () → Agent | flee within 0.3, else toward the nearest visible pellet, else still | BASE-07 |
29	| 14 | `RandomSwimmer` | baselines.random_swimmer | n_agents, seed → Agent | uniform over the unit disc, for either team | BASE-08 |
30	| 15 | `train` | train.loop | Env, Agent, RunSettings → RunRecord | batch-first step loop over every team, building one Transition per learning team and calling its `learn` after each step; each learner's generation every tenth of the run (SELF-04); `evaluate.evaluate` every `eval_every` into a CurvePoint; progress line every 30 s; checkpoints and 5 recorded games at the end | CORE-02, CORE-07, RUN-02–RUN-04, SELF-01, SELF-04, QR-01–QR-03, QR-07 |
31	| 16 | `OpponentPool` | selfplay.pool | Agent, latest_share, seed → OpponentPool | always the latest policy (rung 2's behaviour) | SELF-03 |
32	
33	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
34	```python
35	def run_train(settings_path: Path, seed: int) -> RunRecord:
36	    settings = load_settings(settings_path)
37	    settings = with_seed(settings, seed)
38	    make = make_env(settings)
39	    agents = make_agent(settings, make())
40	    return train(make, agents, settings)
41	```

107	    curves = open_curve_runs(targets)

133	<!-- docs/architecture.md:142 -->
134	### AD-09: A batch-first environment interface over agents, adopted before M1 is built
135	**Status:** Accepted · **Date:** 2026-10-17 · **Scope:** `core`
136	- **Context:** CORE-01 now needs one interface for Snake (one agent, 3 discrete actions) and the fish tank (up to 16 fish, 2-D continuous actions). CORE-06 needs Snake and the corridor to behave as before. M1's plan is approved but no code exists yet.
137	- **Decision:** Every environment is batch-first over its agents. Observations are `(n_agents, *obs_shape)`, actions `(n_agents,)` discrete or `(n_agents, dim)` continuous, per `core.ActionSpace`. Rewards and per-agent `done` are `(n_agents,)`, and the game-level `terminated`/`truncated` stay booleans. Snake and the corridor are the `n_agents == 1` case. `core.Agent.act` takes and returns batches, and `core.Transition` carries per-agent arrays with an `active` mask. M1 adopts this interface before it is built: a Replan of its interface task, so Snake is never written against the old shape.
138	- **Alternatives:** A separate multi-agent protocol beside the single-agent one — two interfaces for `train`, `evaluate` and `replay` to handle, against CORE-07. Per-agent dicts (PettingZoo style) — flexible, but slow for 8 fish in NumPy and unneeded with a fixed agent count. Migrating after M1 is built — the honest "revise the core" lesson, at the cost of rewriting a finished milestone's core and agents.
139	- **Consequences:** AD-13 adds teams on top: each team is batch-first as above. Value learners and Snake baselines handle a batch of 1. CORE-06 is met by M1's golden cases passing on the new interface, with their expected values unchanged. `pipeline-design`, `test-planning` and `build-planning` revisit M1's types and plan before the build starts.
140	
141	<!-- docs/architecture.md:171 -->
142	### AD-13: Teams in the core, adopted before M1 is built
143	**Status:** Accepted · **Date:** 2026-10-17 · **Scope:** `core`
144	- **Context:** CORE-01 now needs environments with two teams of different sizes and views: predator-prey's 1-agent predator (19 values) and 6-agent prey (18 values). AD-09's batch-first arrays assume one observation shape per environment. M1 is approved but not yet built.
145	- **Decision:** `core.Env` declares `teams: Mapping[str, TeamSpec]`. Observations, actions, rewards and `done` are maps from team name to AD-09's batch-first arrays. Single-team environments use one team, `core.SOLO` (`"agents"`). One `core.Agent` acts for one team, and `train` and `evaluate` take one agent per team. `info` carries `score:<team>` per team, plus `score` for the first. M1 adopts this before it's built: a Replan of its interface task.
146	- **Alternatives:** A separate two-team protocol beside `core.Env` — two code paths in `train`, `evaluate` and replay, against CORE-07. Padding both teams to one observation shape with a team flag — one array, but a policy would see the other team's padding, and the scripted players get fiddly. Adding teams after M1 — rewrites M1's loop and evaluation.
147	- **Consequences:** M1's Snake and corridor code addresses `SOLO`; its golden values don't change. M2's fish tank is also one team. S3 adds no second loop.
148	
149	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:21 -->
150	### core.TeamSpec: type
151	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class TeamSpec: n_agents: int; obs_shape: tuple[int, ...]; actions: ActionSpace`
152	- **Invariants:** `n_agents ≥ 1`; `obs_shape` is per agent. A team's agents all share one view and one action space, so one `core.Agent` can act for the whole team.
153	- **Boundary:** agents, baselines, reference, train, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.TeamSpec/ · **Cases:** Snake's one compact-view agent; the tank's 8 full-view fish; predator-prey's predator (1 × 19) and prey (6 × 18) teams
154	- **Used by:** envs, agents, baselines, reference, train, lab; PL-01, PL-03
155	
156	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:27 -->
157	### core.SOLO: Single-team name
158	`SOLO: str = "agents"`
159	- **Invariants:** the one team name every single-team environment uses (Snake, the corridor, the fish tank).
160	- **Boundary:** envs, train, evaluate, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.SOLO/ · **Cases:** the constant
161	- **Used by:** envs, train, evaluate, lab; PL-01, PL-03
162	
163	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:33 -->
164	### core.ActionSpace: type
165	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class ActionSpace: kind: Literal["discrete", "continuous"]; n: int = 0; dim: int = 0`
166	- **Invariants:** discrete: `n ≥ 2` and `dim == 0`. Continuous: `dim ≥ 1`, `n == 0`, and the environment clips each action itself (FISH-02).
167	- **Boundary:** agents, baselines, reference, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.ActionSpace/ · **Cases:** Snake's 3 discrete actions; the tank's 2-D continuous swim vector
168	- **Used by:** envs, agents, baselines, reference, lab; PL-01, PL-03
169	
170	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:7 -->
171	### core.Env: type
172	```python
173	class Env(Protocol):
174	    teams: Mapping[str, TeamSpec]
175	    board: Literal["grid", "tank"]
176	    def reset(self, seed: int) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float]]: ...
177	    def step(self, actions: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> StepResult: ...
178	    def frame(self) -> np.ndarray: ...
179	```
180	- **Invariants:** teams, each batch-first (AD-09, AD-13): observations map each team name to float32 `(n_agents, *obs_shape)` from its `TeamSpec`, and `actions` maps each team to `(n_agents,)` int64 when discrete or `(n_agents, dim)` float32 when continuous. Single-team environments have one team, `"agents"` (`core.SOLO`). Entries for agents already done are ignored. `info` always holds `steps`, `score:<team>` for each team, and `score` for the first team. `frame()` is an int8 grid of cell codes (0 empty, 1 head, 2 body, 3 food or goal) when `board == "grid"`, and a `(K, 4)` float32 array of circles `(x, y, radius, kind)` when `board == "tank"`, with kinds 0 ball, 1 fish, 2 caught fish, 3 predator, 4 prey, 5 caught prey, 6 pellet (AD-12). The same seed and actions give the same results.
181	- **Errors:** `RuntimeError` if `step` is called after the game is over. `ValueError` for a missing team, an action of the wrong shape, outside the discrete range, or non-finite.
182	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, reference, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Env/ · **Cases:** a recorded 20-step Snake game ending in a wall hit; a recorded 9-step corridor game reaching the goal; a recorded 30-step 8-fish tank game with 2 catches; a recorded 40-step predator-prey game with 1 catch and 2 pellets eaten
183	- **Used by:** envs (provider), train, evaluate, reference, lab; PL-01, PL-03, PL-04, PL-05, PL-06
184	
185	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:39 -->
186	### core.StepResult: type
187	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class StepResult: obs: dict[str, np.ndarray]; reward: dict[str, np.ndarray]; done: dict[str, np.ndarray]; terminated: bool; truncated: bool; info: dict[str, float]`
188	- **Invariants:** each map has one entry per team. `reward[team]` is `(n_agents,)` float32 and `done[team]` `(n_agents,)` bool, true while an agent is out (a collision, the goal, a catch, or a caught prey waiting to reappear). `terminated` means the game itself ended (every single-team agent done); `truncated` means a step cap or starvation ended the game first. At most one of the two is true. `info` holds `steps`, `score:<team>` per team and `score`.
189	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, reference · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.StepResult/ · **Cases:** an ordinary step; eating food; a wall collision; starvation truncation; a tank step with one fish caught; the tank's 1,000-step truncation; a predator-prey step with a catch
190	- **Used by:** envs, train, evaluate, reference; PL-01, PL-03
191	
192	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:45 -->
193	### core.Transition: type
194	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Transition: obs: np.ndarray; actions: np.ndarray; reward: np.ndarray; next_obs: np.ndarray; terminated: np.ndarray; active: np.ndarray; truncated: bool`
195	- **Invariants:** one `Transition` per learning team per step, every array batch-first over that team's agents. `active` marks agents that acted this step. `terminated` is true per agent only for that agent's real end (a collision, the corridor goal or a catch), never for truncation, so truncated steps still bootstrap.
196	- **Boundary:** agents, baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Transition/ · **Cases:** a food step; a collision step; a truncated step; a tank step with 8 active fish of which one is caught
197	- **Used by:** train, agents, baselines; PL-01
198	
199	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:51 -->
200	### core.Agent: type
201	```python
202	class Agent(Protocol):
203	    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, *, explore: bool) -> np.ndarray: ...
204	    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> None: ...
205	    def save(self, path: Path) -> None: ...
206	    def load(self, path: Path) -> None: ...
207	```
208	- **Invariants:** one agent acts for one team: `act` takes that team's `(n_agents, *obs_shape)` and returns one action per agent, shaped by the team's `ActionSpace`. `explore=False` never samples or takes a random action (AGENT-07): value learners take the best-valued action and policy learners the policy's mean. Players that don't learn make `learn` a no-op. `save` then `load` gives the same `act` outputs.
209	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Agent/ · **Cases:** a replay double recording 5 act/learn calls of a tabular agent
210	- **Used by:** agents, baselines, reference (providers); train, evaluate, lab; PL-01, PL-03, PL-04, PL-05
211	
212	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:63 -->
213	### core.RunSettings: type
214	```python
215	@dataclass(frozen=True)
216	class RunSettings:
217	    name: str; env: Literal["snake", "corridor", "tank", "predprey"]; view: Literal["compact", "grid", "index", "full", "limited"]
218	    agent: Literal["tabular", "dqn", "reinforce", "ppo", "random", "scripted", "reference"]; n_agents: int
219	    opponents: Mapping[str, Literal["learn", "scripted", "random"]]; self_play: Literal["none", "latest", "pool"]
220	    pool_latest: float; generations: int
221	    ablation: Literal["none", "no_memory", "no_target", "no_explore"]
222	    rewards: Rewards; exploration: Exploration; params: AgentParams
223	    steps: int; seed: int; eval_every: int; eval_games: int
224	```
225	- **Invariants:** `seed` in `[0, 1_000_000)`. `steps` > 0. `view` belongs to `env` (Snake `compact`/`grid`, corridor `index`, tank `full`/`limited`). `n_agents` is 1 except for the tank (1–16, FISH-07). `reinforce` and `ppo` only with the tank; `tabular` and `dqn` only with Snake or the corridor. `reference` on the tank needs `n_agents == 1` and `full`. For `predprey`, `view` is `limited`, `agent` is `ppo` or `reference`, and `opponents` names both teams (`predator`, `prey`): `learn` teams train with `agent`; `self_play` is `latest` or `pool` only when both learn (rung 2, rung 3), `none` otherwise; `generations` is 10 when any team learns, else 0; `pool_latest` defaults to 0.5 (SELF-03). Single-team runs have `opponents == {}`. `ablation` matches `agent` (`no_memory` and `no_target` with `dqn`, `no_explore` with `tabular`).
226	- **Boundary:** lab, train, runs, reference · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.RunSettings/ · **Cases:** the dqn-compact ladder settings; a corridor tabular settings; a no_target ablation; the rung-3 tank settings (ppo, 8 fish, full view); the tank reference settings; predator-prey rung 1 (prey learn, scripted predator); rung 3 (both learn, pool)
227	- **Used by:** lab, train, runs, reference, agents; PL-01, PL-02, PL-03, PL-04, PL-06
228	
229	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:79 -->
230	### core.Rewards: type
231	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Rewards: food: float = 1.0; death: float = -1.0; step: float = 0.0`
232	- **Invariants:** for the corridor, `food` is the goal reward. For the tank, `step` is the reward per surviving fish-step and `death` the reward on a catch; tank settings default to `step = 1.0`, `death = 0.0` (FISH-05).
233	- **Boundary:** envs · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Rewards/ · **Cases:** defaults; food set to +10; the tank's survival rewards
234	- **Used by:** envs, lab; PL-01, PL-02
235	
236	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:85 -->
237	### core.Exploration: type
238	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Exploration: start: float = 1.0; end: float = 0.05; decay_steps: int = 50_000`
239	- **Invariants:** `0 ≤ end ≤ start ≤ 1`. The rate falls linearly to `end` at `decay_steps`, then stays there (AGENT-02).
240	- **Boundary:** agents, reference · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Exploration/ · **Cases:** defaults; no decay (`start == end`)
241	- **Used by:** agents, reference; PL-01, PL-02
242	
243	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:91 -->
244	### core.AgentParams: type
245	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class AgentParams: lr: float; gamma: float; batch_size: int = 64; memory_size: int = 50_000; target_every: int = 1_000; learn_start: int = 1_000; hidden: int = 128; net: Literal["mlp", "cnn"] = "mlp"; rollout_steps: int = 2048; epochs: int = 10; clip: float = 0.2; gae_lambda: float = 0.95; games_per_update: int = 10`
246	- **Invariants:** `0 < gamma ≤ 1`. The table-based agent reads only `lr` (its step size) and `gamma`. Policy-gradient agents read `lr`, `gamma`, `hidden` and `batch_size`; PPO also `rollout_steps`, `epochs`, `clip`, `gae_lambda`; REINFORCE also `games_per_update` (AD-11).
247	- **Boundary:** agents, reference · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.AgentParams/ · **Cases:** the dqn-compact defaults; tabular `lr=0.1, gamma=0.9`; the ppo defaults (`lr=3e-4, gamma=0.99, hidden=64`)
248	- **Used by:** agents, reference; PL-01, PL-02
249	
250	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:270 -->
251	### runs.CurvePoint: type
252	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class CurvePoint: step: int; games: int; mean_score: float; elapsed_s: float`
253	- **Invariants:** `step` strictly increases within a run. `mean_score` comes from a greedy evaluation of `eval_games` games.
254	- **Boundary:** train, reference, viz · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.CurvePoint/ · **Cases:** the first point at step 0; a mid-run point

258	### runs.GameRecording: type
259	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class GameRecording: seed: int; board: Literal["grid", "tank"]; frames: np.ndarray; rewards: np.ndarray; scores: dict[str, np.ndarray]`
260	- **Invariants:** `frames` holds the start frame plus one per step: T×H×W int8 for `grid`, T×K×4 float32 circles for `tank` (AD-12). `rewards` is the per-step total over all agents, length T−1; `scores` maps each team to its score at every frame, length T.

265	### runs.RunRecord: type
266	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class RunRecord: path: Path; settings: RunSettings; curve: tuple[CurvePoint, ...]; status: Literal["running", "complete", "incomplete"]; checkpoints: dict[str, Path]; generations: dict[str, tuple[Path, ...]]; games: tuple[GameRecording, ...]`
267	- **Invariants:** `settings` round-trips through `settings.json` unchanged. `checkpoints` holds one final checkpoint per learning team (single-team runs: `SOLO`), present when `status` is `complete`. `generations` holds each learning team's numbered generations in order (10 when `settings.generations == 10`, else empty) (RUN-02, SELF-04).

272	### runs.RunWriter: Run folder writer
273	```python
274	class RunWriter:
275	    def add_point(self, p: CurvePoint) -> None: ...
276	    def save_checkpoint(self, agent: Agent, *, team: str = SOLO) -> None: ...
277	    def save_generation(self, agent: Agent, *, team: str, index: int) -> Path: ...
278	    def save_games(self, games: Sequence[GameRecording]) -> None: ...
279	    def finish(self, status: Literal["complete", "incomplete"]) -> RunRecord: ...
280	```
281	- **Invariants:** each `add_point` is flushed to `curve.csv` at once, so an interrupt keeps the curve. Generations are written as `generations/<team>/<index>` and returned, so a pool can load them during the run. The folder layout follows AD-06.
282	- **Boundary:** train, reference · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.RunWriter/ · **Cases:** a replay double recording a 3-point run that finishes complete

340	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:106 -->
341	### envs.Snake: Snake game
342	```python
343	class Snake(Env):
344	    def __init__(self, *, view: Literal["compact", "grid"], rewards: Rewards = Rewards(), size: int = 10) -> None: ...
345	    def reset_to(self, body: Sequence[tuple[int, int]], heading: Literal["up", "right", "down", "left"], food: tuple[int, int]) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float]]: ...
346	```
347	- **Invariants:** SNAKE-01–SNAKE-09. `reset_to` starts a game from a given board (cells are `(row, col)`, row 0 at the top, body head first), with score 0 and the step count at 0, and returns what `reset` does (AD-13); later food is seeded as after `reset`. One team, `SOLO`, of 1 agent; `board == "grid"`. Actions: discrete 3, 0 straight, 1 turn left, 2 turn right. The compact observation has per-agent shape `(11,)`, laid out as `COMPACT_FIELDS`. The grid observation has per-agent shape `(3, size, size)`: head, body and food channels. Filling the board ends the game with `terminated=True` and no death reward.
348	- **Errors:** as `core.Env`; `reset_to` raises `ValueError` if the body is not contiguous, leaves the grid, or the food is on the snake
349	- **Boundary:** lab, reference, baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.Snake/ · **Cases:** a recorded compact-view game ending at a wall; a recorded grid-view game eating twice then hitting itself
350	- **Used by:** lab, reference, baselines; PL-01, PL-03
351

354	`class Corridor(Env): def __init__(self, *, length: int = 10, max_steps: int = 50, rewards: Rewards = Rewards())`
355	- **Invariants:** CORE-03. One team, `SOLO`, of 1 agent; `board == "grid"`. Actions: discrete 2, 0 left, 1 right. The observation is a one-hot position of per-agent shape `(length,)`. `frame()` is `1 × length`.
356	- **Errors:** as `core.Env`
357	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.Corridor/ · **Cases:** a recorded 9-step game reaching the goal; a 50-step truncated game
358	- **Used by:** lab; PL-01, PL-03

375	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:220 -->
376	### agents.TabularAgent: Table-based value learner
377	`class TabularAgent(Agent): def __init__(self, n_actions: int, params: AgentParams, exploration: Exploration, *, seed: int, explore_enabled: bool = True)`
378	- **Invariants:** single-agent (batches of 1). One value per (observation, action), keyed on the observation's bytes and starting at 0. Each `learn` moves the value toward `reward + gamma · max next value`, with no bootstrap when `terminated` (AGENT-01). `explore_enabled=False` is the `no_explore` ablation (ABL-03). The checkpoint is NPZ.
379	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/agents.TabularAgent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of 5 act/learn calls on the corridor
380	- **Used by:** lab; PL-01, PL-03
381	
382	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:226 -->
383	### agents.DQNAgent: Neural value learner
384	`class DQNAgent(Agent): def __init__(self, team: TeamSpec, params: AgentParams, exploration: Exploration, *, seed: int, ablation: Literal["none", "no_memory", "no_target"] = "none")`
385	- **Invariants:** single-agent (batches of 1), its observation shape and action count from `team` (AD-18). AGENT-03–AGENT-05 and AD-05. With `ablation="no_memory"`, each update uses only the latest transition (ABL-01). With `"no_target"`, targets come from the online network (ABL-02). No updates happen before `learn_start` transitions. The checkpoint is a PyTorch state dict.
386	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `team` has more than one agent or continuous actions, or if `net="cnn"` and `team.obs_shape` is not 3-D
387	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/agents.DQNAgent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of 5 act/learn calls on the compact view after `learn_start`
388	- **Used by:** lab; PL-01, PL-03
389

408	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `n` > 1000 or `train_seed` ≥ 1,000,000

413	### evaluate.evaluate: Greedy evaluation
414	`def evaluate(make_env: Callable[[], Env], agents: Mapping[str, Agent], *, player: str, train_seed: int, options: EvalOptions = EvalOptions()) -> ScoreSet`
415	- **Invariants:** `agents` has one agent per team of the environment; the scores are `options.team`'s `score:<team>`. Plays `options.n_games` games on `eval_seeds(train_seed, options.n_games)` with `explore=False`. It never calls `learn`. It records the first `options.record` games (REPLAY-03). 200 games of any environment take ≤ 60 s on the laptop (QR-04).
416	- **Boundary:** train, reference, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.evaluate/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the scripted-player ScoreSet for seed 0
417	- **Used by:** train, reference, lab; PL-01, PL-02, PL-03, PL-04, PL-05

419	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:342 -->
420	### train.train: Training loop
421	`def train(make_env: Callable[[], Env], agents: Mapping[str, Agent], settings: RunSettings, *, root: Path = Path("runs")) -> RunRecord`
422	- **Invariants:** `agents` has one agent per team; the teams `settings.opponents` marks `learn` (or `SOLO` in a single-team run) are learners. Steps the environment for `settings.steps` steps, building one batch-first `Transition` per learning team and calling that team's `learn` after every step (CORE-07). With `settings.generations`, it saves each learner's generation every tenth of the run (SELF-04); with `self_play == "pool"`, each game's opponent for every learner comes from its `selfplay.OpponentPool` (SELF-03, AD-14). Curve points evaluate the first learning team against the run's fixed opposition. It evaluates every `eval_every` steps (including step 0) into a `CurvePoint`. It prints a progress line at least every 30 s (RUN-04). It saves each learner's checkpoint and 5 recorded evaluation games at the end. The same settings give the same curve (RUN-03). It knows nothing environment-specific (CORE-02).
423	- **Errors:** on `KeyboardInterrupt`, finishes the run `incomplete`, then re-raises
424	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.train/ · **Cases:** a fake returning a complete 3-point corridor RunRecord
425	- **Used by:** lab; PL-01
426	
427	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:351 -->
428	### reference.train_reference: Library agent training
429	`def train_reference(settings: RunSettings, *, root: Path = Path("runs")) -> RunRecord`
430	- **Invariants:** `settings.agent == "reference"`. Trains Stable-Baselines3 DQN on Snake (AD-04), or PPO on the single-fish tank or on the predator-prey predator against scripted prey (AD-10, AD-15), through the AD-03 adapter with `device="cpu"` and the matching one-to-one settings mapping. Curve points use `evaluate.evaluate` at the same `eval_every`. The checkpoint loads into `ReferencePlayer`.
431	- **Errors:** `ValueError` unless the settings are Snake with the compact view, the tank with one fish and the full view, or predator-prey with `opponents == {"predator": "learn", "prey": "scripted"}`
432	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/reference.train_reference/ · **Cases:** a fake returning a complete 3-point reference RunRecord
433	- **Used by:** lab; PL-02
434

Writes `out/report.md` listing every player's mean and spread (BASE-03) and every claim, with stretch claims labelled "stretch" (EVAL-05), and links each chart image in `charts` by its path under a "Cross-play" section (EVAL-11). `compare` never imports `viz` (AD-18).

458	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:475 -->
459	### lab.main: Command line
460	`def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int`
461	- **Invariants:** subcommands are `train <settings.toml> [--seeds 0-4]`, `eval <run | random | scripted>… [--env snake|tank|predprey] [--team T]`, `replay <run> [--game N | --new --seed S | --pair <predator gen> <prey gen>]`, `crossplay <run>` and `report <report.toml>`. `train` builds the env and agent from the settings alone (RUN-01), and runs reference settings through `train_reference`. An ablation settings file carries its full agent's step budget, and `report` refuses an ablation whose seeds or steps differ from its full agent's (ABL-04). Returns 0 on success, 2 on bad input.
462	- **Errors:** bad settings and missing runs print the `ValueError` or `FileNotFoundError` message and return 2
463	- **Used by:** the learner; PL-01, PL-02, PL-03, PL-04, PL-05, PL-06

465	<!-- docs/architecture.md:99 -->
466	### AD-03: Own environment protocol, shaped like Gymnasium
467	**Status:** Accepted · **Date:** 2026-10-17 · **Scope:** `core`
468	- **Context:** CORE-01 needs one interface for every environment. REF-01 needs the library agent to train on the same Snake. AGENT-06 keeps library code out of the lab's agents.
469	- **Decision:** `core.Env` is a small Protocol whose `reset(seed)` returns `(obs, info)` and whose `step(actions)` returns a `StepResult` with Gymnasium's fields (observation, reward, terminated, truncated, info), plus `frame()` for replay. AD-09 sets their batch-first shape over agents. `reference` wraps one single-agent team of a `core.Env`, with every other team played by a frozen agent (AD-15), in a `gymnasium.Env` adapter.
470	- **Alternatives:** Subclass `gymnasium.Env` directly — no adapter, but every environment and the core then depend on Gymnasium. A bespoke interface unlike Gymnasium's — needs a bigger adapter and teaches a nonstandard shape.
471	- **Consequences:** Truncation (starvation or the step cap) and termination (a collision or a catch) stay separate, so learning targets bootstrap correctly on truncation. The adapter is checked with Stable-Baselines3's `check_env`.
472	
473	<!-- docs/architecture.md:121 -->
474	### AD-06: One folder per run; TOML settings in, plain files out
475	**Status:** Accepted · **Date:** 2026-10-17 · **Scope:** `runs`
476	- **Context:** RUN-01 needs a run fully described by its settings. RUN-02 needs settings, seed, curve, checkpoint and games saved. RUN-05 needs reloading. The interrupted-run edge case needs partial records kept.
477	- **Decision:** Settings are read from TOML into a frozen `core.RunSettings`. Each run writes `runs/<name>-s<seed>-<timestamp>/` holding `settings.json` (the resolved settings), `curve.csv`, `checkpoint.pt` or `checkpoint.npz`, `games.npz` and `status` (`running`, `complete` or `incomplete`). The curve is appended as the run goes. An interrupt marks the run `incomplete`.
478	- **Alternatives:** An experiment tracker such as MLflow or W&B — a server or account for a one-person lab. One SQLite file — harder to inspect by eye and to delete one run from.
479	- **Consequences:** A run is inspectable with any text editor and deleted by removing its folder. Nothing indexes runs, so reports name their run folders explicitly.
480

488	- src/rllab/evaluate/** (folder)

57	## Review
58	Run by the reviewer agent over what it is given (base, package, tasks, paths): the interface review after Phase 0, a phase review per package at each checkpoint, and the milestone exit. `dt land` already checked each landing's `Writes:`, protected paths and gates; review judges what tools can't.
59	
60	**Interface** (after Phase 0): the code is the contract, no more and no less.
61	1. Types match `contract.md`: fields, types, invariants. Nothing a step's In → Out needs is missing; no helper is frozen.
62	2. Signatures match the step tables, in their owner modules. Each stub names the open task that owns its step; no logic beyond types and validation.
63	3. One sample per case the contract names, valid and realistically shaped, never carrying a TC's expected value. Replay doubles replay an interaction the Protocol allows; fakes serve their fixtures.
64	4. `__init__.py` exports exactly the contract; each `Mitigated by` in the plan's dependency graph exists; each split task's tester can write its tests from the frozen interface and its `Read:`.
65	
66	**Each phase**, per package, tests before code:
67	1. **Interfaces followed** (CR-09): no frozen type, signature or contract folder changed outside a Revise. Consumers use the declared types: no dict for a dataclass, no reaching into a provider's internals, no local redefinition. A stub is replaced only by the task it names; no cross-package import outside the contract; spine tests run on samples and doubles, not another step's code.
68	2. **Tests prove something:** each intent test asserts its TC case or acceptance bullet, loading expected values and tolerances from fixtures; none is assertion-free or weakened; Hypothesis runs the `ci` profile. Unit tests are specific, never computing the expected value with the code under test or testing only a mock. **Mutation check** (`.claude/agents/reviewer.md`): per acceptance bullet, the smallest change that should break it; a mutation no test catches is a finding.
69	3. **Correct to the spec:** each acceptance bullet truly met, not special-cased to the sample; the error paths the requirement names; seeding and determinism; numerical edge cases.
70	4. **Simple** (CR-11, CR-12, CR-42): no abstraction with one implementation outside the contract or an AD; no forwarding wrapper, one-method class, or class where a function does; no parameter, flag or config key every caller sets the same; no `try/except` that only re-raises or swallows, no checks the types already guarantee; no loop the library already provides. No near-copies within or across the phase's tasks: one concept is one function in its owner module (tests may repeat for readability). Each finding names the simpler form, with the same tests passing.
71	5. **Pipelines** (CR-10): entry functions read as their step tables (`dt layout`); step owners respected; spine baselines still selectable.
72	6. **The bar** (CR-07): diff this file, `pyproject.toml`, `scripts/` and `.claude/agents/` against the milestone's Base. Any loosening is Critical; new exceptions need an owner and expiry.
73	7. **Hidden and parallel risks:** where a real provider could act differently from its double (flagged for the system phase); fixed output paths, global state, test-order dependence.
74	8. **The rest:** readability, security, performance where a QR sets a number; reused reference or probe code has its Caveats handled and fits the contract; `dt lint` clean, pre-build sections replaced by `dt layout` pointers.
75	
76	**Milestone exit**, over the whole diff, adds: the Scope's Exit holds; every Covers ID has a test; `check.sh full --exit` finds no stub; system tests use real providers; the same sample through a real provider and its double agrees; deferred requirements untouched; pip-audit ran.
77	
78	**Severity:** Critical: a frozen interface changed, a weakened test, an intent test that misses a mutation changing its TC's expected output, a wrong answer, a loosened bar, a security hole, lost data; it stops the build. Important: a consumer bypassing a type, any other surviving mutation, an unneeded abstraction, a duplicate across tasks. Suggestion and Nit: local. Findings go under the checkpoint block in `tasks/plan.md` (`dt finding`), each with a fix task or a reason.

7	#   scripts/check.sh collect             tester's finish gate            (Stop: tester)
8	#   scripts/check.sh gate [--pkg ID]     implementer's finish gate       (Stop: implementer and solo; dt land)
9	#   scripts/check.sh full [--pkg ID]     checkpoints, CI                 (reviewer)
10	#   scripts/check.sh full --exit         the milestone exit: also no interface stub left
11	#   scripts/check.sh protected           print the protected-path globs  (path_guard.sh deny --protected)

13	# The base is refs/worktree/checks-base, one per worktree (dt claim sets it in a builder's worktree,
14	# dt land in the main checkout), else HEAD.

25	# deptry too); gate and full also run tests/contracts, whole, when it exists. Lint, types, size

42	# from the architecture's dependency rules.

51	    train) echo "train" ;;

54	    viz) echo "viz" ;;
55	    lab) echo "lab" ;;
56	    *) return 1 ;;
57	  esac

93	# A tool from the project's locked environment, never whatever is first on PATH.
94	run() {
95	  echo "-> $*" >&2

97	}

99	# A measured check that fails only at the milestone exit: it prints its findings, and the reviewer
100	# records them, but a mid-milestone run passes.
101	report() {
102	  echo "-> $* (reported; fails only with --exit)" >&2
103	  uv run --frozen --quiet "$@" || echo "(reported, not failing: $*)" >&2
104	}

106	# pytest exits 5 when it collects nothing. That is fine for gate and full on a project with no
107	# tests yet; collect calls pytest through run() so an empty tests/intent fails.
108	run_tests() {
109	  echo "-> pytest $*" >&2
110	  uv run --frozen --quiet pytest "$@"
111	  local status=$?
112	  [ "$status" -eq 5 ] && { echo "(no tests collected)" >&2; return; }
113	  [ "$status" -eq 0 ] || { rc=1; failed+=("pytest $*"); }
114	}

116	# The commit this task started from: refs/worktree/checks-base, set per worktree by `dt claim` (and
117	# by `dt land` in the main checkout). Without it, HEAD, which misses changes already committed.
118	BASE_REF="refs/worktree/checks-base"
119	base_commit() {
120	  git rev-parse -q --verify "$BASE_REF" 2>/dev/null || git rev-parse -q --verify HEAD 2>/dev/null
121	}
122	
123	# A boundary contract is frozen once its folder exists at the base (CR-09): each folder under
124	# tests/contracts/ at the base, and any file directly in it. A folder new since the base is the
125	# interface task freezing it, so that task may write it; the next task's base freezes it. A frozen
126	# folder the task's .claude/.task_writes names (from its Writes:, by dt claim) is a re-freeze
127	# interface task's, so it is open to that task; dt land checks the Writes: against the plan.
128	CONTRACTS_GLOB="tests/contracts/**"
129	claimed_contract() {
130	  local line
131	  [ -s .claude/.task_writes ] || return 1
132	  while IFS= read -r line; do
133	    line="${line%%[[:space:]]}"
134	    line="${line%/}"; line="${line%/\*\*}"
135	    [ "$line" = "$1" ] && return 0
136	  done <.claude/.task_writes
137	  return 1
138	}
139	frozen_contracts() {
140	  local base meta path
141	  base=$(base_commit) || return 0
142	  git ls-tree "$base" -- tests/contracts/ 2>/dev/null | while IFS=$'\t' read -r meta path; do
143	    case "$meta" in
144	      *" tree "*) claimed_contract "$path" || echo "$path/**" ;;
145	      *) echo "$path" ;;
146	    esac
147	  done
148	}
149	
150	# The protected list as enforced: PROTECTED, with tests/contracts/** narrowed to the frozen contracts.
151	protected_globs() {
152	  local g
153	  for g in "${PROTECTED[@]}"; do
154	    if [ "$g" = "$CONTRACTS_GLOB" ]; then frozen_contracts; else echo "$g"; fi
155	  done
156	}
157	
158	# Fail if anything matching the given pathspecs changed since the base, untracked files included.
159	unchanged_since_base() {
160	  local label="$1" base new
161	  shift
162	  echo "-> $label unchanged since $(git rev-parse -q --verify "$BASE_REF" >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo "$BASE_REF" || echo HEAD)" >&2
163	  if ! base=$(base_commit); then
164	    echo "no commit to compare against: commit the tester's work first" >&2
165	    rc=1; failed+=("$label: no commit"); return
166	  fi
167	  git --no-pager diff --exit-code --stat "$base" -- "$@" >&2 || { rc=1; failed+=("$label changed"); }
168	  new=$(git ls-files --others --exclude-standard -- "$@")
169	  if [ -n "$new" ]; then
170	    printf 'new untracked files in %s:\n%s\n' "$label" "$new" >&2
171	    rc=1; failed+=("$label changed")
172	  fi
173	}
174	
175	# The tester may change tests/intent only. Docs and plans are the plugin's business, not this check's.
176	only_intent_changed() {
177	  local specs=(tests "${SRC_DIRS[@]}" "${PROTECTED[@]}")
178	  [ -n "$PACKAGES_DIR" ] && specs+=("$PACKAGES_DIR")
179	  unchanged_since_base "files outside tests/intent" "${specs[@]}" ':(exclude)tests/intent'
180	}
181	
182	# The intent test files the tester changed since the base, committed or not.
183	changed_intent_tests() {
184	  local base
185	  base=$(base_commit) || return 0
186	  { git diff --relative --name-only --diff-filter=d "$base" -- 'tests/intent/*.py'
187	    git ls-files --others --exclude-standard -- 'tests/intent/*.py'; } | sort -u |
188	    while IFS= read -r f; do case "${f##*/}" in test_*|*_test.py) echo "$f" ;; esac; done
189	}
190	
191	# Test directories for this run: all of them, or the --pkg scope; tests/contracts always whole.
192	test_dirs() {
193	  local scope="" d p
194	  [ -n "$pkg" ] && scope=$(pkg_scope "$pkg")
195	  for d in tests/intent tests/unit; do
196	    if [ -z "$scope" ]; then
197	      [ -d "$d" ] && echo "$d"
198	    else
199	      for p in $scope; do [ -d "$d/$p" ] && echo "$d/$p"; done
200	    fi
201	  done
202	  [ -d tests/contracts ] && echo tests/contracts
203	}
204	
205	# deptry checks what each package imports against what it declares.
206	dependencies() {
207	  local p scope
208	  if [ -z "$PACKAGES_DIR" ]; then
209	    run deptry . --no-ansi
210	    return
211	  fi
212	  if [ -n "$pkg" ]; then
213	    scope=$(pkg_scope "$pkg")
214	  else
215	    scope=$(cd "$PACKAGES_DIR" && for d in */pyproject.toml; do [ -f "$d" ] && dirname "$d"; done)
216	  fi
217	  for p in $scope; do
218	    echo "-> deptry $PACKAGES_DIR/$p" >&2
219	    (cd "$PACKAGES_DIR/$p" && uv run --frozen --quiet deptry . --no-ansi) || { rc=1; failed+=("deptry $PACKAGES_DIR/$p"); }
220	  done
221	}
222	
223	static_checks() {
224	  run ruff check --ignore-noqa .   # exceptions go in per-file-ignores, which is protected (CR-01)
225	  run ruff format --check .
226	  run "$TYPE_GATE"
227	  run python scripts/check_size.py "${SRC_DIRS[@]}"
228	  dependencies
229	  run lint-imports --no-logo
230	}
231	
232	case "$mode" in
233	  fast)
234	    # Only Python files, only the files just edited. Fixes what it can in place.
235	    files=()
236	    for f in ${args[@]+"${args[@]}"}; do [[ $f == *.py ]] && files+=("$f"); done
237	    [ ${#files[@]} -eq 0 ] && exit 0
238	    run ruff check --fix --quiet "${files[@]}"
239	    run ruff format --quiet "${files[@]}"
240	    run typos "${files[@]}"
241	    run python scripts/check_size.py "${files[@]}"
242	    ;;
243	  collect)
244	    if [ ! -d tests/intent ]; then
245	      echo "tests/intent does not exist: write the intent tests there" >&2
246	      exit 1
247	    fi
248	    run ruff check --ignore-noqa tests/intent
249	    run ruff format --check tests/intent
250	    run typos tests/intent
251	    run pytest tests/intent --collect-only -q --no-cov
252	    changed=()
253	    while IFS= read -r f; do changed+=("$f"); done < <(changed_intent_tests)
254	    run python scripts/red_check.py ${changed[@]+"${changed[@]}"}
255	    step bash scripts/floor_guard.sh
256	    only_intent_changed
257	    ;;
258	  gate)
259	    static_checks
260	    other_parts
261	    dirs=$(test_dirs)
262	    # shellcheck disable=SC2086  # one directory per word, by construction
263	    [ -n "$dirs" ] && run_tests $dirs --no-cov -q -x
264	    step bash scripts/floor_guard.sh
265	    protected=()
266	    while IFS= read -r g; do protected+=("$g"); done < <(protected_globs)
267	    unchanged_since_base "protected files" "${protected[@]}"
268	    ;;
269	  full)
270	    static_checks
271	    other_parts
272	    if [ "$TYPE_GATE" = mypy ]; then run pyright; else run mypy; fi
273	    # CR-41: the interface task's stubs and fields stay unused until later tasks use them.
274	    if [ ${#exit_check[@]} -gt 0 ]; then run vulture; else report vulture; fi
275	    run pylint --disable=all --enable=duplicate-code --recursive=y --score=n --fail-under=10 "${SRC_DIRS[@]}"   # CR-42
276	    run typos
277	    run pip-audit --skip-editable   # the external opinion: a vulnerability database (CR-26)
278	    step bash scripts/floor_guard.sh ${exit_check[@]+"${exit_check[@]}"}
279	    dirs=$(test_dirs)
280	    # Coverage threshold comes from [tool.coverage.report] fail_under (CR-40).
281	    # shellcheck disable=SC2086
282	    [ -n "$dirs" ] && HYPOTHESIS_PROFILE=ci run_tests $dirs --cov -q
283	    ;;
284	  protected)
285	    protected_globs
286	    ;;
287	  *)
288	    echo "unknown mode: $mode (fast | collect | gate | full | protected)" >&2
289	    exit 64
290	    ;;
291	esac
292	[ $rc -ne 0 ] && printf 'FAILED: %s\n' "${failed[@]}" >&2
293	exit $rc

1	# rllab — Contract
2	
3	**Package:** rllab · **Status:** Approved · **Last updated:** 2026-10-18 · **Architecture:** [../../architecture.md](../../architecture.md)
4	
5	## core
6	
7	### core.Env: type
8	```python
9	class Env(Protocol):
10	    teams: Mapping[str, TeamSpec]
11	    board: Literal["grid", "tank"]
12	    def reset(self, seed: int) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float]]: ...
13	    def step(self, actions: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> StepResult: ...
14	    def frame(self) -> np.ndarray: ...
15	```
16	- **Invariants:** teams, each batch-first (AD-09, AD-13): observations map each team name to float32 `(n_agents, *obs_shape)` from its `TeamSpec`, and `actions` maps each team to `(n_agents,)` int64 when discrete or `(n_agents, dim)` float32 when continuous. Single-team environments have one team, `"agents"` (`core.SOLO`). Entries for agents already done are ignored. `info` always holds `steps`, `score:<team>` for each team, and `score` for the first team. `frame()` is an int8 grid of cell codes (0 empty, 1 head, 2 body, 3 food or goal) when `board == "grid"`, and a `(K, 4)` float32 array of circles `(x, y, radius, kind)` when `board == "tank"`, with kinds 0 ball, 1 fish, 2 caught fish, 3 predator, 4 prey, 5 caught prey, 6 pellet (AD-12). The same seed and actions give the same results.
17	- **Errors:** `RuntimeError` if `step` is called after the game is over. `ValueError` for a missing team, an action of the wrong shape, outside the discrete range, or non-finite.
18	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, reference, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Env/ · **Cases:** a recorded 20-step Snake game ending in a wall hit; a recorded 9-step corridor game reaching the goal; a recorded 30-step 8-fish tank game with 2 catches; a recorded 40-step predator-prey game with 1 catch and 2 pellets eaten
19	- **Used by:** envs (provider), train, evaluate, reference, lab; PL-01, PL-03, PL-04, PL-05, PL-06
20	
21	### core.TeamSpec: type
22	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class TeamSpec: n_agents: int; obs_shape: tuple[int, ...]; actions: ActionSpace`
23	- **Invariants:** `n_agents ≥ 1`; `obs_shape` is per agent. A team's agents all share one view and one action space, so one `core.Agent` can act for the whole team.
24	- **Boundary:** agents, baselines, reference, train, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.TeamSpec/ · **Cases:** Snake's one compact-view agent; the tank's 8 full-view fish; predator-prey's predator (1 × 19) and prey (6 × 18) teams
25	- **Used by:** envs, agents, baselines, reference, train, lab; PL-01, PL-03
26	
27	### core.SOLO: Single-team name
28	`SOLO: str = "agents"`
29	- **Invariants:** the one team name every single-team environment uses (Snake, the corridor, the fish tank).
30	- **Boundary:** envs, train, evaluate, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.SOLO/ · **Cases:** the constant
31	- **Used by:** envs, train, evaluate, lab; PL-01, PL-03
32	
33	### core.ActionSpace: type
34	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class ActionSpace: kind: Literal["discrete", "continuous"]; n: int = 0; dim: int = 0`
35	- **Invariants:** discrete: `n ≥ 2` and `dim == 0`. Continuous: `dim ≥ 1`, `n == 0`, and the environment clips each action itself (FISH-02).
36	- **Boundary:** agents, baselines, reference, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.ActionSpace/ · **Cases:** Snake's 3 discrete actions; the tank's 2-D continuous swim vector
37	- **Used by:** envs, agents, baselines, reference, lab; PL-01, PL-03
38	
39	### core.StepResult: type
40	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class StepResult: obs: dict[str, np.ndarray]; reward: dict[str, np.ndarray]; done: dict[str, np.ndarray]; terminated: bool; truncated: bool; info: dict[str, float]`
41	- **Invariants:** each map has one entry per team. `reward[team]` is `(n_agents,)` float32 and `done[team]` `(n_agents,)` bool, true while an agent is out (a collision, the goal, a catch, or a caught prey waiting to reappear). `terminated` means the game itself ended (every single-team agent done); `truncated` means a step cap or starvation ended the game first. At most one of the two is true. `info` holds `steps`, `score:<team>` per team and `score`.
42	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, reference · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.StepResult/ · **Cases:** an ordinary step; eating food; a wall collision; starvation truncation; a tank step with one fish caught; the tank's 1,000-step truncation; a predator-prey step with a catch
43	- **Used by:** envs, train, evaluate, reference; PL-01, PL-03
44	
45	### core.Transition: type
46	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Transition: obs: np.ndarray; actions: np.ndarray; reward: np.ndarray; next_obs: np.ndarray; terminated: np.ndarray; active: np.ndarray; truncated: bool`
47	- **Invariants:** one `Transition` per learning team per step, every array batch-first over that team's agents. `active` marks agents that acted this step. `terminated` is true per agent only for that agent's real end (a collision, the corridor goal or a catch), never for truncation, so truncated steps still bootstrap.
48	- **Boundary:** agents, baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Transition/ · **Cases:** a food step; a collision step; a truncated step; a tank step with 8 active fish of which one is caught
49	- **Used by:** train, agents, baselines; PL-01
50	
51	### core.Agent: type
52	```python
53	class Agent(Protocol):
54	    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, *, explore: bool) -> np.ndarray: ...
55	    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> None: ...
56	    def save(self, path: Path) -> None: ...
57	    def load(self, path: Path) -> None: ...
58	```
59	- **Invariants:** one agent acts for one team: `act` takes that team's `(n_agents, *obs_shape)` and returns one action per agent, shaped by the team's `ActionSpace`. `explore=False` never samples or takes a random action (AGENT-07): value learners take the best-valued action and policy learners the policy's mean. Players that don't learn make `learn` a no-op. `save` then `load` gives the same `act` outputs.
60	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Agent/ · **Cases:** a replay double recording 5 act/learn calls of a tabular agent
61	- **Used by:** agents, baselines, reference (providers); train, evaluate, lab; PL-01, PL-03, PL-04, PL-05
62	
63	### core.RunSettings: type
64	```python
65	@dataclass(frozen=True)
66	class RunSettings:
67	    name: str; env: Literal["snake", "corridor", "tank", "predprey"]; view: Literal["compact", "grid", "index", "full", "limited"]
68	    agent: Literal["tabular", "dqn", "reinforce", "ppo", "random", "scripted", "reference"]; n_agents: int
69	    opponents: Mapping[str, Literal["learn", "scripted", "random"]]; self_play: Literal["none", "latest", "pool"]
70	    pool_latest: float; generations: int
71	    ablation: Literal["none", "no_memory", "no_target", "no_explore"]
72	    rewards: Rewards; exploration: Exploration; params: AgentParams
73	    steps: int; seed: int; eval_every: int; eval_games: int
74	```
75	- **Invariants:** `seed` in `[0, 1_000_000)`. `steps` > 0. `view` belongs to `env` (Snake `compact`/`grid`, corridor `index`, tank `full`/`limited`). `n_agents` is 1 except for the tank (1–16, FISH-07). `reinforce` and `ppo` only with the tank; `tabular` and `dqn` only with Snake or the corridor. `reference` on the tank needs `n_agents == 1` and `full`. For `predprey`, `view` is `limited`, `agent` is `ppo` or `reference`, and `opponents` names both teams (`predator`, `prey`): `learn` teams train with `agent`; `self_play` is `latest` or `pool` only when both learn (rung 2, rung 3), `none` otherwise; `generations` is 10 when any team learns, else 0; `pool_latest` defaults to 0.5 (SELF-03). Single-team runs have `opponents == {}`. `ablation` matches `agent` (`no_memory` and `no_target` with `dqn`, `no_explore` with `tabular`).
76	- **Boundary:** lab, train, runs, reference · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.RunSettings/ · **Cases:** the dqn-compact ladder settings; a corridor tabular settings; a no_target ablation; the rung-3 tank settings (ppo, 8 fish, full view); the tank reference settings; predator-prey rung 1 (prey learn, scripted predator); rung 3 (both learn, pool)
77	- **Used by:** lab, train, runs, reference, agents; PL-01, PL-02, PL-03, PL-04, PL-06
78	
79	### core.Rewards: type
80	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Rewards: food: float = 1.0; death: float = -1.0; step: float = 0.0`
81	- **Invariants:** for the corridor, `food` is the goal reward. For the tank, `step` is the reward per surviving fish-step and `death` the reward on a catch; tank settings default to `step = 1.0`, `death = 0.0` (FISH-05).
82	- **Boundary:** envs · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Rewards/ · **Cases:** defaults; food set to +10; the tank's survival rewards
83	- **Used by:** envs, lab; PL-01, PL-02
84	
85	### core.Exploration: type
86	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Exploration: start: float = 1.0; end: float = 0.05; decay_steps: int = 50_000`
87	- **Invariants:** `0 ≤ end ≤ start ≤ 1`. The rate falls linearly to `end` at `decay_steps`, then stays there (AGENT-02).
88	- **Boundary:** agents, reference · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Exploration/ · **Cases:** defaults; no decay (`start == end`)
89	- **Used by:** agents, reference; PL-01, PL-02
90	
91	### core.AgentParams: type
92	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class AgentParams: lr: float; gamma: float; batch_size: int = 64; memory_size: int = 50_000; target_every: int = 1_000; learn_start: int = 1_000; hidden: int = 128; net: Literal["mlp", "cnn"] = "mlp"; rollout_steps: int = 2048; epochs: int = 10; clip: float = 0.2; gae_lambda: float = 0.95; games_per_update: int = 10`
93	- **Invariants:** `0 < gamma ≤ 1`. The table-based agent reads only `lr` (its step size) and `gamma`. Policy-gradient agents read `lr`, `gamma`, `hidden` and `batch_size`; PPO also `rollout_steps`, `epochs`, `clip`, `gae_lambda`; REINFORCE also `games_per_update` (AD-11).
94	- **Boundary:** agents, reference · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.AgentParams/ · **Cases:** the dqn-compact defaults; tabular `lr=0.1, gamma=0.9`; the ppo defaults (`lr=3e-4, gamma=0.99, hidden=64`)
95	- **Used by:** agents, reference; PL-01, PL-02
96	
97	### core.load_settings: Read run settings
98	`def load_settings(path: Path) -> RunSettings`
99	- **Invariants:** unspecified fields take the dataclass defaults. The result is fully resolved, so no other input is needed (RUN-01).
100	- **Errors:** `ValueError` naming the key for an unknown environment, view, agent, ablation, or an out-of-range value. `FileNotFoundError` if the file is missing.
101	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.load_settings/ · **Cases:** the dqn-compact ladder file; a file naming an unknown agent
102	- **Used by:** lab; PL-01, PL-02
103	
104	## envs
105	
106	### envs.Snake: Snake game
107	```python
108	class Snake(Env):
109	    def __init__(self, *, view: Literal["compact", "grid"], rewards: Rewards = Rewards(), size: int = 10) -> None: ...
110	    def reset_to(self, body: Sequence[tuple[int, int]], heading: Literal["up", "right", "down", "left"], food: tuple[int, int]) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float]]: ...
111	```
112	- **Invariants:** SNAKE-01–SNAKE-09. `reset_to` starts a game from a given board (cells are `(row, col)`, row 0 at the top, body head first), with score 0 and the step count at 0, and returns what `reset` does (AD-13); later food is seeded as after `reset`. One team, `SOLO`, of 1 agent; `board == "grid"`. Actions: discrete 3, 0 straight, 1 turn left, 2 turn right. The compact observation has per-agent shape `(11,)`, laid out as `COMPACT_FIELDS`. The grid observation has per-agent shape `(3, size, size)`: head, body and food channels. Filling the board ends the game with `terminated=True` and no death reward.
113	- **Errors:** as `core.Env`; `reset_to` raises `ValueError` if the body is not contiguous, leaves the grid, or the food is on the snake
114	- **Boundary:** lab, reference, baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.Snake/ · **Cases:** a recorded compact-view game ending at a wall; a recorded grid-view game eating twice then hitting itself
115	- **Used by:** lab, reference, baselines; PL-01, PL-03
116	
117	### envs.Corridor: 1-D corridor
118	`class Corridor(Env): def __init__(self, *, length: int = 10, max_steps: int = 50, rewards: Rewards = Rewards())`
119	- **Invariants:** CORE-03. One team, `SOLO`, of 1 agent; `board == "grid"`. Actions: discrete 2, 0 left, 1 right. The observation is a one-hot position of per-agent shape `(length,)`. `frame()` is `1 × length`.
120	- **Errors:** as `core.Env`
121	- **Boundary:** lab · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.Corridor/ · **Cases:** a recorded 9-step game reaching the goal; a 50-step truncated game
122	- **Used by:** lab; PL-01, PL-03
123	
124	### envs.COMPACT_FIELDS: Compact view layout
125	`COMPACT_FIELDS: tuple[str, ...] = ("danger_straight", "danger_left", "danger_right", "head_up", "head_right", "head_down", "head_left", "food_left", "food_right", "food_up", "food_down")`
126	- **Invariants:** index `i` of a compact observation is field `i`. Every value is 0.0 or 1.0.
127	- **Boundary:** baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.COMPACT_FIELDS/ · **Cases:** the 11-name tuple
128	- **Used by:** baselines; PL-03
129	
130	### envs.FishTank: Fish tank
131	```python
132	class FishTank(Env):
133	    def __init__(self, *, view: Literal["full", "limited"], n_fish: int = 8, rewards: Rewards = Rewards(food=0.0, death=0.0, step=1.0)) -> None: ...
134	    def reset_to(self, fish: Sequence[tuple[float, float]], ball: tuple[float, float], ball_velocity: tuple[float, float]) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float]]: ...
135	```
136	- **Invariants:** FISH-01–FISH-10 with the spec's numbers as module constants. One team, `SOLO`, of `n_fish` agents; actions are continuous with `dim == 2`; `board == "tank"`. The full view has per-fish shape `(8,)` laid out as `TANK_FULL_FIELDS`, and the limited view `(9,)` as `TANK_LIMITED_FIELDS`. `info["score"]` is the mean survival time per fish so far. `reset_to` starts from given positions, at step 0.
137	- **Errors:** as `core.Env`; `reset_to` raises `ValueError` for a position outside the tank or a fish within 0.11 of the ball
138	- **Boundary:** lab, reference, baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.FishTank/ · **Cases:** a recorded 30-step 8-fish full-view game with 2 catches; a recorded single-fish limited-view game reaching the 1,000-step cap
139	- **Used by:** lab, reference, baselines; PL-01, PL-03
140	
141	### envs.TANK_FULL_FIELDS: Tank full-view layout
142	`TANK_FULL_FIELDS: tuple[str, ...] = ("x", "y", "vx", "vy", "ball_dx", "ball_dy", "ball_vx", "ball_vy")`
143	- **Invariants:** index `i` of a full-view observation is field `i`. `ball_dx`, `ball_dy` are the ball's centre minus the fish's.
144	- **Boundary:** baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.TANK_FULL_FIELDS/ · **Cases:** the 8-name tuple
145	- **Used by:** baselines; PL-03
146	
147	### envs.TANK_LIMITED_FIELDS: Tank limited-view layout
148	`TANK_LIMITED_FIELDS: tuple[str, ...] = ("x", "y", "vx", "vy", "ball_visible", "ball_dx", "ball_dy", "ball_vx", "ball_vy")`
149	- **Invariants:** the five ball fields are 0 unless the ball's centre is within 0.5 of the fish (FISH-10).
150	- **Boundary:** agents · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.TANK_LIMITED_FIELDS/ · **Cases:** the 9-name tuple
151	- **Used by:** agents; PL-01
152	
153	### envs.PredatorPrey: Predator-prey world
154	```python
155	class PredatorPrey(Env):
156	    def __init__(self, *, rewards: Rewards = Rewards(food=1.0, death=-5.0, step=0.0)) -> None: ...
157	    def reset_to(self, predator: tuple[float, float], prey: Sequence[tuple[float, float]], pellets: Sequence[tuple[float, float]]) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float]]: ...
158	```
159	- **Invariants:** HUNT-01–HUNT-09, with the spec's numbers as module constants and the tank's vectorised physics (AD-16). Teams `"predator"` (1 agent, view `(19,)` as `PREDATOR_FIELDS`) and `"prey"` (6 agents, view `(18,)` as `PREY_FIELDS`), both continuous with `dim == 2`; `board == "tank"`. `rewards.food` is the prey's pellet reward and `rewards.death` its catch penalty; the predator earns +1 per catch. `info` holds `score:predator` (catches so far) and `score:prey` (mean reward per prey so far). The game only truncates, at step 1,000.
160	- **Errors:** as `core.Env`; `reset_to` raises `ValueError` for a position outside the tank, a count other than 6 prey or 8 pellets, or a prey within 0.4 of the predator
161	- **Boundary:** lab, reference, baselines, selfplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.PredatorPrey/ · **Cases:** a recorded 40-step game with 1 catch and 2 pellets eaten; a recorded 60-step game where a caught prey reappears
162	- **Used by:** lab, reference, baselines, selfplay; PL-01, PL-03, PL-06
163	
164	### envs.PREY_FIELDS: Prey view layout
165	`PREY_FIELDS: tuple[str, ...] = ("x", "y", "vx", "vy", "pred_visible", "pred_dx", "pred_dy", "pred_vx", "pred_vy", "p1_visible", "p1_dx", "p1_dy", "p2_visible", "p2_dx", "p2_dy", "p3_visible", "p3_dx", "p3_dy")`
166	- **Invariants:** HUNT-05: pellets ordered nearest first; out-of-range slots are 0 with their flag 0.
167	- **Boundary:** baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.PREY_FIELDS/ · **Cases:** the 18-name tuple
168	- **Used by:** baselines; PL-01, PL-03
169	
170	### envs.PREDATOR_FIELDS: Predator view layout
171	`PREDATOR_FIELDS: tuple[str, ...] = ("x", "y", "vx", "vy", "q1_visible", "q1_dx", "q1_dy", "q1_vx", "q1_vy", "q2_visible", "q2_dx", "q2_dy", "q2_vx", "q2_vy", "q3_visible", "q3_dx", "q3_dy", "q3_vx", "q3_vy")`
172	- **Invariants:** HUNT-06: swimming prey ordered nearest first; out-of-range slots are 0 with their flag 0.
173	- **Boundary:** baselines · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/envs.PREDATOR_FIELDS/ · **Cases:** the 19-name tuple
174	- **Used by:** baselines; PL-01, PL-03
