126	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- the diff
127	HUNK = re.compile(r"^@@ -\d+(?:,\d+)? \+(\d+)(?:,\d+)? @@")
128	
129	
130	def _diff_path(raw: str) -> str:
131	    raw = raw.rstrip("\t")
132	    return raw[2:] if raw[:2] in {"a/", "b/"} else raw
133	
134	
135	def parse_diff(text: str) -> Diff:
136	    """Added lines (with new line numbers), removed lines and deleted files, per file."""
137	    diff = Diff()
138	    old = new = ""
139	    line_no = 0
140	    in_header = False
141	    for line in text.splitlines():
142	        if line.startswith("diff --git "):
143	            old, new, in_header = "", "", True
144	        elif in_header and line.startswith("--- "):
145	            old = _diff_path(line[4:])
146	        elif in_header and line.startswith("+++ "):
147	            new = _diff_path(line[4:])
148	            if new == "/dev/null":
149	                diff.deleted.add(old)
150	        elif match := HUNK.match(line):
151	            in_header, line_no = False, int(match[1])
152	        elif in_header:
153	            continue
154	        elif line.startswith("+"):
155	            diff.added.setdefault(new, []).append((line_no, line[1:]))
156	            line_no += 1
157	        elif line.startswith("-"):
158	            diff.removed.setdefault(old, []).append(line[1:])
159	    return diff
160	
161	
162	def add_untracked(diff: Diff, paths: list[str]) -> None:
163	    """An untracked file is all added lines."""
164	    for name in paths:
165	        path = Path(name)
166	        if not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size > 2_000_000:
167	            continue
168	        try:
169	            text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
170	        except (UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
171	            continue
172	        diff.added[name] = list(enumerate(text.splitlines(), start=1))
173	
174	
175	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- line checks
176	def is_outside(path: str) -> bool:
177	    return path.startswith(tuple(f"{folder}/" for folder in OUTSIDE))
178	
179	
180	def is_python(path: str) -> bool:
181	    if path.startswith("scripts/") or is_outside(path):
182	        return False
183	    return path.endswith((".py", ".pyi"))
184	
185	
186	def is_test_file(path: str) -> bool:
187	    parts = Path(path).parts
188	    name = parts[-1] if parts else ""
189	    return (
190	        "tests" in parts
191	        and (name.startswith("test_") or name.endswith("_test.py"))
192	        and not is_outside(path)
193	    )
194	
195	
196	def line_findings(diff: Diff) -> list[Finding]:
197	    found: list[Finding] = []
198	    for path, lines in diff.added.items():
199	        python, test = is_python(path), is_test_file(path)
200	        path_settings = not is_outside(path) and not path.endswith(DOC_SUFFIXES)
201	        for n, text in lines:
202	            if python and SUPPRESSION.search(text):
203	                found.append(Finding("suppression", path, n, "new suppression comment"))
204	            if path_settings and IMPORT_PATH_SETTING.search(text):
205	                found += [
206	                    Finding(f"{folder}-path", path, n, f"{folder}/ on an import path")
207	                    for folder, word in OUTSIDE.items()
208	                    if word.search(text)
209	                ]
210	            if test and SKIP.search(text):
211	                found.append(Finding("test-skipped", path, n, "test skipped or marked xfail"))
212	            if Path(path).name not in LOCKFILES:
213	                found += [
214	                    Finding("secret", path, n, f"looks like a {kind} (value not shown)")
215	                    for kind, pattern in SECRETS.items()
216	                    if pattern.search(text)
217	                ]
218	    return found
219	
220	
221	def test_file_findings(diff: Diff) -> list[Finding]:
222	    found = [
223	        Finding("test-deleted", p, 0, "test file deleted") for p in diff.deleted if is_test_file(p)
224	    ]
225	    for path, lines in diff.removed.items():
226	        if not is_test_file(path) or path in diff.deleted:
227	            continue
228	        lost = sum(bool(ASSERTION.search(t)) for t in lines)
229	        gained = sum(bool(ASSERTION.search(t)) for _, t in diff.added.get(path, []))
230	        if lost > gained:
231	            found.append(
232	                Finding("assertion-removed", path, 0, f"{lost - gained} assertion line(s) removed")
233	            )
234	    return found
235	
236	
237	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- AST checks
238	def _touches(node: ast.AST, lines: set[int]) -> bool:
239	    start = getattr(node, "lineno", 0)
240	    end = getattr(node, "end_lineno", start) or start
241	    return any(start <= n <= end for n in lines)
242	
243	
244	def _is_trivial(body: list[ast.stmt]) -> bool:
245	    """Only pass, `...` or `raise NotImplementedError`, after an optional docstring."""
246	    stmts = body[1:] if body and _is_docstring(body[0]) else body
247	    return all(_is_placeholder(s) for s in stmts)
248	
249	
250	def _task_stub(body: list[ast.stmt]) -> str | None:
251	    """The task an interface stub names: a body that is only `raise NotImplementedError("M1-T05")`."""
252	    stmts = body[1:] if body and _is_docstring(body[0]) else body
253	    if len(stmts) != 1 or not isinstance(stmts[0], ast.Raise):
254	        return None
255	    exc = stmts[0].exc
256	    if not (isinstance(exc, ast.Call) and _name(exc.func) == "NotImplementedError"):
257	        return None
258	    arg = exc.args[0] if len(exc.args) == 1 and not exc.keywords else None
259	    if (
260	        isinstance(arg, ast.Constant)
261	        and isinstance(arg.value, str)
262	        and TASK_ID.fullmatch(arg.value)
263	    ):
264	        return arg.value
265	    return None
266	
267	
268	def _is_docstring(stmt: ast.stmt) -> bool:
269	    return (
270	        isinstance(stmt, ast.Expr)
271	        and isinstance(stmt.value, ast.Constant)
272	        and isinstance(stmt.value.value, str)
273	    )
274	
275	
276	def _is_placeholder(stmt: ast.stmt) -> bool:
277	    if isinstance(stmt, ast.Pass):
278	        return True
279	    if isinstance(stmt, ast.Expr) and isinstance(stmt.value, ast.Constant):
280	        return stmt.value.value is Ellipsis
281	    if isinstance(stmt, ast.Raise) and stmt.exc is not None:
282	        exc = stmt.exc.func if isinstance(stmt.exc, ast.Call) else stmt.exc
283	        return _name(exc) == "NotImplementedError"
284	    return False
285	
286	
287	def _name(node: ast.AST) -> str | None:
288	    if isinstance(node, ast.Name):
289	        return node.id
290	    if isinstance(node, ast.Attribute):
291	        return node.attr
292	    if isinstance(node, ast.Call):
293	        return _name(node.func)
294	    return None
295	
296	
297	def _exempt_functions(tree: ast.Module) -> set[ast.AST]:
298	    """Abstract methods, overloads and Protocol members may have placeholder bodies."""
299	    exempt: set[ast.AST] = set()
300	    for node in ast.walk(tree):
301	        if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
302	            if {_name(d) for d in node.decorator_list} & EXEMPT_DECORATORS:
303	                exempt.add(node)
304	        elif isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef) and "Protocol" in {_name(b) for b in node.bases}:
305	            exempt.update(
306	                n for n in node.body if isinstance(n, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef))
307	            )
308	    return exempt
309	
310	
311	def _in_folder(name: str | None, folder: str) -> bool:
312	    return name is not None and (name == folder or name.startswith(f"{folder}."))
313	
314	
315	def _mentions(node: ast.AST, word: re.Pattern[str]) -> bool:
316	    return any(
317	        isinstance(n, ast.Constant) and isinstance(n.value, str) and word.search(n.value)
318	        for n in ast.walk(node)
319	    )
320	
321	
322	def _is_sys_path(node: ast.AST) -> bool:
323	    return (
324	        isinstance(node, ast.Attribute)
325	        and node.attr == "path"
326	        and isinstance(node.value, ast.Name)
327	        and node.value.id == "sys"
328	    )
329	
330	
331	def _imported_modules(node: ast.AST) -> list[str | None]:
332	    """Absolute module names an import statement or an import_module call brings in."""
333	    if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
334	        return [alias.name for alias in node.names]
335	    if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):
336	        return [node.module] if node.level == 0 else []
337	    if isinstance(node, ast.Call) and _name(node.func) in {"import_module", "__import__"}:
338	        first = node.args[0] if node.args else None
339	        if isinstance(first, ast.Constant) and isinstance(first.value, str):
340	            return [first.value]
341	    return []
342	
343	
344	def _changes_sys_path(node: ast.AST) -> bool:
345	    """sys.path.insert/append/extend, site.addsitedir, or an assignment to sys.path."""
346	    if isinstance(node, ast.Call) and isinstance(node.func, ast.Attribute):
347	        func = node.func
348	        return func.attr == "addsitedir" or (
349	            func.attr in SYS_PATH_CALLS and _is_sys_path(func.value)
350	        )
351	    if isinstance(node, ast.Assign):
352	        return any(_is_sys_path(target) for target in node.targets)
353	    if isinstance(node, ast.AugAssign):
354	        return _is_sys_path(node.target)
355	    return False
356	
357	
358	def _outside_finding(path: str, node: ast.AST) -> Finding | None:
359	    """CR-08: an import from references/ or probes/, or either folder added to sys.path."""
360	    line = getattr(node, "lineno", 0)
361	    for folder, word in OUTSIDE.items():
362	        if any(_in_folder(name, folder) for name in _imported_modules(node)):
363	            return Finding(f"{folder}-import", path, line, f"import from {folder}/")
364	        if _changes_sys_path(node) and _mentions(node, word):
365	            return Finding(f"{folder}-path", path, line, f"{folder}/ added to sys.path")
366	    return None
367	
368	
369	def _asserts(func: ast.AST) -> bool:
370	    for node in ast.walk(func):
371	        if isinstance(node, ast.Assert):
372	            return True
373	        name = _name(node) if isinstance(node, ast.Call) else None
374	        if name and (
375	            name.startswith("assert") or name in {"raises", "warns", "deprecated_call", "fail"}
376	        ):
377	            return True
378	    return False
379	
380	
381	def ast_findings(path: str, lines: set[int]) -> list[Finding]:
382	    try:
383	        tree = ast.parse(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
384	    except (SyntaxError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
385	        return []  # ruff reports syntax errors; a deleted file has nothing to check
386	    exempt, test = _exempt_functions(tree), is_test_file(path)
387	    found: list[Finding] = []
388	    for node in ast.walk(tree):
389	        if not _touches(node, lines):
390	            continue
391	        if outside := _outside_finding(path, node):
392	            found.append(outside)
393	        if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
394	            if (
395	                node not in exempt
396	                and not path.endswith(".pyi")
397	                and _is_trivial(node.body)
398	                and not _task_stub(node.body)
399	            ):
400	                found.append(
401	                    Finding("stub", path, node.lineno, f"`{node.name}` has a placeholder body")
402	                )
403	            if test and node.name.startswith("test") and not _asserts(node):
404	                found.append(
405	                    Finding(
406	                        "test-without-assertion",
407	                        path,
408	                        node.lineno,
409	                        f"`{node.name}` asserts nothing",
410	                    )
411	                )
412	        elif isinstance(node, ast.ExceptHandler) and _is_trivial(node.body):
413	            if (None if node.type is None else _name(node.type)) in TRIVIAL_EXCEPT:
414	                found.append(
415	                    Finding("swallowed-exception", path, node.lineno, "exception swallowed")
416	                )
417	    return found
418	
419	
420	def open_tasks() -> set[str]:
421	    """Task IDs with an unchecked line in tasks/todo.md (none when there is no todo)."""
422	    path = Path("tasks/todo.md")
423	    return set(OPEN_TASK.findall(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))) if path.is_file() else set()
424	
425	
426	def task_stub_findings(paths: list[str], at_exit: bool) -> list[Finding]:
427	    """Interface stubs anywhere in the project's code: each must name an open task, and at the
428	    milestone exit none may be left. Not diff-scoped: the stub was written by an earlier task."""
429	    open_ids, found = open_tasks(), []
430	    for path in paths:
431	        if not is_python(path) or is_test_file(path) or not Path(path).is_file():
432	            continue
433	        try:
434	            tree = ast.parse(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
435	        except (SyntaxError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
436	            continue
437	        for node in ast.walk(tree):
438	            if not isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
439	                continue
440	            if not (task := _task_stub(node.body)):
441	                continue
442	            if at_exit:
443	                found.append(
444	                    Finding(
445	                        "stub-left",
446	                        path,
447	                        node.lineno,
448	                        f"`{node.name}` is still the stub for {task}",
449	                    )
450	                )
451	            elif task not in open_ids:
452	                found.append(
453	                    Finding(
454	                        "stub-task",
455	                        path,
456	                        node.lineno,
457	                        f"`{node.name}` is a stub naming {task}, which is not open in tasks/todo.md",
458	                    )
459	                )
460	    return found
461	
462	
463	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- the bar: tool config
464	MAX_KEYS = (
465	    "tool.ruff.lint.mccabe.max-complexity",
466	    "tool.size_limits.max_module_lines",
467	    "tool.size_limits.max_function_lines",
468	    "tool.vulture.min_confidence",
469	    "tool.pylint.similarities.min-similarity-lines",
470	)
471	MIN_KEYS = ("tool.coverage.report.fail_under", "tool.size_limits.min_module_lines")
472	TRUE_KEYS = ("tool.mypy.strict", "tool.pytest.ini_options.xfail_strict")
473	OFF_KEYS = ("tool.pylint.main.exit-zero",)  # turning one on silences a check
474	NO_SHRINK = ("tool.ruff.lint.select", "tool.ruff.lint.extend-select")
475	NO_GROW = (
476	    "tool.ruff.lint.ignore", "tool.ruff.lint.extend-ignore", "tool.ruff.exclude",
477	    "tool.ruff.extend-exclude", "tool.ruff.lint.exclude", "tool.deptry.exclude",
478	    "tool.deptry.extend_exclude", "tool.deptry.ignore", "tool.vulture.ignore_names",
479	    "tool.vulture.exclude", "tool.mypy.exclude", "tool.pylint.main.ignore-paths",
480	    "tool.pylint.main.ignore", "tool.pylint.main.ignore-patterns",
481	)  # fmt: skip
482	# Excluding the references or probes folder is not a loosening: neither is project code (CR-08).
483	OUTSIDE_EXCLUDE = re.compile(r"\^?(references|probes)/?\*{0,2}")
484	NO_GROW_TABLES = ("tool.ruff.lint.per-file-ignores", "tool.deptry.per_rule_ignores")
485	MUST_MATCH = ("tool.importlinter", "tool.mypy.overrides", "tool.pyright")
486	STRICT_FLAGS = ("--strict-markers", "--strict-config")
487	
488	
489	def _get(data: dict[str, Any], dotted: str) -> Any:
490	    for key in dotted.split("."):
491	        if not isinstance(data, dict) or key not in data:
492	            return None
493	        data = data[key]
494	    return data
495	
496	
497	def _as_set(value: Any) -> set[str]:
498	    if isinstance(value, str):
499	        return {value}
500	    return {str(v) for v in value} if isinstance(value, list) else set()
501	
502	
503	def _limit_findings(old: dict[str, Any], new: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
504	    pylint = _get(old, "tool.ruff.lint.pylint") or {}
505	    maxes = [*MAX_KEYS, *(f"tool.ruff.lint.pylint.{k}" for k in pylint)]
506	    out = [
507	        f"{k}: {_get(old, k)} -> {_get(new, k)}"
508	        for k in maxes
509	        if _loosened(_get(old, k), _get(new, k), +1)
510	    ]
511	    out += [
512	        f"{k}: {_get(old, k)} -> {_get(new, k)}"
513	        for k in MIN_KEYS
514	        if _loosened(_get(old, k), _get(new, k), -1)
515	    ]
516	    out += [
517	        f"{k} turned off" for k in TRUE_KEYS if _get(old, k) is True and _get(new, k) is not True
518	    ]
519	    out += [f"{k} turned on" for k in OFF_KEYS if _get(new, k) is True and _get(old, k) is not True]
520	    return out
521	
522	
523	def _loosened(was: Any, now: Any, direction: int) -> bool:
524	    if not isinstance(was, (int, float)):
525	        return False
526	    if not isinstance(now, (int, float)):
527	        return True  # a limit that disappeared
528	    return (now - was) * direction > 0
529	
530	
531	def _list_findings(old: dict[str, Any], new: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
532	    out = [f"{k} lost {sorted(_as_set(_get(old, k)) - _as_set(_get(new, k)))}" for k in NO_SHRINK]
533	    for k in NO_GROW:
534	        gained = _as_set(_get(new, k)) - _as_set(_get(old, k))
535	        out.append(f"{k} gained {sorted(g for g in gained if not OUTSIDE_EXCLUDE.fullmatch(g))}")
536	    for table in NO_GROW_TABLES:
537	        was, now = _get(old, table) or {}, _get(new, table) or {}
538	        out += [
539	            f"{table}.{key} gained {sorted(_as_set(codes) - _as_set(was.get(key)))}"
540	            for key, codes in now.items()
541	            if _as_set(codes) - _as_set(was.get(key))
542	        ]
543	    flags = _as_set((_get(new, "tool.pytest.ini_options.addopts") or "").split())
544	    out += [
545	        f"pytest addopts dropped {f}"
546	        for f in STRICT_FLAGS
547	        if f in str(_get(old, "tool.pytest.ini_options.addopts")) and f not in flags
548	    ]
549	    out += [
550	        f"{k} changed (boundaries and type config change only through build-setup)"
551	        for k in MUST_MATCH
552	        if _get(old, k) != _get(new, k)
553	    ]
554	    return [o for o in out if not o.endswith("[]")]
555	
556	
557	def config_findings(old_pyproject: Path, diff: Diff) -> list[Finding]:
558	    found = [
559	        Finding("bar-loosened", p, 0, "new tool config file overrides pyproject.toml")
560	        for p in diff.added
561	        if Path(p).name in CONFIG_FILES and not p.startswith("scripts/")
562	    ]
563	    old_text = old_pyproject.read_text(encoding="utf-8") if old_pyproject.is_file() else ""
564	    if not old_text or not Path("pyproject.toml").is_file():
565	        return found
566	    try:
567	        old, new = (
568	            tomllib.loads(old_text),
569	            tomllib.loads(Path("pyproject.toml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")),
570	        )
571	    except tomllib.TOMLDecodeError as exc:
572	        return [*found, Finding("bar-loosened", "pyproject.toml", 0, f"cannot parse: {exc}")]
573	    changes = _limit_findings(old, new) + _list_findings(old, new)
574	    return found + [Finding("bar-loosened", "pyproject.toml", 0, c) for c in changes]
575	
576	
577	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- CONSTRAINTS.md
578	RULE_REF = re.compile(r"^\s*(?:-\s*\*\*(CR-\d+)\*\*|\|\s*(CR-\d+)\s*\|)")
579	
580	
581	def _section(lines: list[str], title: str) -> tuple[int, int]:
582	    """1-based line range of a `## title` section, or (0, -1)."""
583	    start = next((i for i, t in enumerate(lines, 1) if t.strip().lower() == f"## {title}"), 0)
584	    if not start:
585	        return 0, -1
586	    end = next(
587	        (i - 1 for i, t in enumerate(lines, 1) if i > start and t.startswith("## ")), len(lines)
588	    )
589	    return start, end
590	
591	
592	def _table_rows(lines: list[str], start: int, end: int) -> list[tuple[int, list[str]]]:
593	    rows = []
594	    for n in range(start + 1, end + 1):
595	        text = lines[n - 1].strip()
596	        if not text.startswith("|"):
597	            continue
598	        cells = [c.strip() for c in text.strip("|").split("|")]
599	        if cells and cells[0] not in {"", "ID"} and not set(cells[0]) <= set("-: "):
600	            rows.append((n, cells))
601	    return rows
602	
603	
604	def constraints_findings(diff: Diff) -> list[Finding]:
605	    path = "CONSTRAINTS.md"
606	    if path not in diff.added and path not in diff.removed:
607	        return []
608	    still = {
609	        m.group(1) or m.group(2) for _, t in diff.added.get(path, []) if (m := RULE_REF.match(t))
610	    }
611	    gone = {
612	        m.group(1) or m.group(2) for t in diff.removed.get(path, []) if (m := RULE_REF.match(t))
613	    }
614	    found = [Finding("rule-removed", path, 0, f"{rule} removed") for rule in sorted(gone - still)]
615	    lines = Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if Path(path).is_file() else []
616	    start, end = _section(lines, "exceptions")
617	    new_rows = diff.added_lines(path)
618	    found += [
619	        Finding("new-exception", path, n, f"new exception {cells[0]} needs the user's yes")
620	        for n, cells in _table_rows(lines, start, end)
621	        if n in new_rows
622	    ]
623	    return found
624	
625	
626	def load_exceptions(today: date) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
627	    """(rule cell, path glob) for each unexpired row in CONSTRAINTS.md's Exceptions table."""
628	    path = Path("CONSTRAINTS.md")
629	    if not path.is_file():
630	        return []
631	    lines = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
632	    allowed = []
633	    for _, cells in _table_rows(lines, *_section(lines, "exceptions")):
634	        if len(cells) < 6:
635	            continue
636	        expires = re.match(r"\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}", cells[5])
637	        if expires and date.fromisoformat(expires[0]) < today:
638	            continue
639	        allowed.append((cells[1], cells[2].strip("`")))
640	    return allowed
641	
642	
643	def is_excepted(finding: Finding, allowed: list[tuple[str, str]]) -> bool:
644	    rule_id = RULE_IDS.get(finding.rule)
645	    return bool(rule_id) and any(
646	        rule_id in rule and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding.path, glob) for rule, glob in allowed
647	    )
648	
649	
650	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- main
651	def run(
652	    diff: Diff, old_pyproject: Path, python_files: list[str] | None = None, at_exit: bool = False
653	) -> list[Finding]:
654	    found = line_findings(diff) + test_file_findings(diff)
655	    found += task_stub_findings(python_files or [], at_exit)
656	    for path in diff.added:
657	        if is_python(path):
658	            found += ast_findings(path, diff.added_lines(path))
659	    found += config_findings(old_pyproject, diff) + constraints_findings(diff)
660	    allowed = load_exceptions(datetime.now(tz=UTC).date())
661	    return [f for f in found if not is_excepted(f, allowed)]
662	
663	
664	def main(argv: list[str]) -> int:
665	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
666	    parser.add_argument("--diff", type=Path, required=True)
667	    parser.add_argument("--untracked", type=Path, required=True)
668	    parser.add_argument("--old-pyproject", type=Path, required=True)
669	    parser.add_argument(
670	        "--python", type=Path, help="every Python file in the project, one per line"
671	    )
672	    parser.add_argument(
673	        "--exit", action="store_true", help="the milestone exit: no stub may be left"
674	    )
675	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
676	    diff = parse_diff(args.diff.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"))
677	    add_untracked(diff, args.untracked.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines())
678	    python_files = args.python.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if args.python else []
679	    findings = run(diff, args.old_pyproject, python_files, args.exit)
680	    if not findings:
681	        print("floor_guard: clean", file=sys.stderr)
682	        return 0
683	    print("\n".join(str(f) for f in sorted(findings)))
684	    print(
685	        f"floor_guard: {len(findings)} floor violation(s). Fix the code; a real exception goes "
686	        "through build-setup (Revise) and the Exceptions table in CONSTRAINTS.md.",
687	        file=sys.stderr,
688	    )
689	    return 1
690	
691	
692	if __name__ == "__main__":
693	    sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))

34	    limits = dict(DEFAULTS)
35	    if pyproject.is_file():
36	        data = tomllib.loads(pyproject.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

41	def iter_files(paths: list[str]) -> list[Path]:
42	    files: list[Path] = []
43	    for raw in paths:
44	        p = Path(raw)
45	        if p.is_dir():
46	            files += [f for f in sorted(p.rglob("*.py")) if not SKIP_DIRS & set(f.parts)]
47	        elif p.suffix == ".py" and p.is_file():
48	            files.append(p)
49	    return files

52	def is_code(line: str) -> bool:
53	    s = line.strip()
54	    return bool(s) and not s.startswith("#")
55	
56	
57	def check_file(path: Path, limits: dict[str, int]) -> list[str]:
58	    source = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
59	    lines = source.splitlines()
60	    problems: list[str] = []
61	
62	    n = sum(is_code(line) for line in lines)
63	    if n > limits["max_module_lines"]:
64	        problems.append(
65	            f"{path}:1: SIZE001 module has {n} code lines (max {limits['max_module_lines']})"
66	        )
67	    if path.name != "__init__.py" and n < limits["min_module_lines"]:
68	        problems.append(
69	            f"{path}:1: SIZE002 module has {n} code lines (min {limits['min_module_lines']})"
70	        )
71	
72	    try:
73	        tree = ast.parse(source)
74	    except SyntaxError as exc:
75	        return [*problems, f"{path}:{exc.lineno or 1}: SIZE000 cannot parse: {exc.msg}"]
76	
77	    for node in ast.walk(tree):
78	        if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
79	            start = min([node.lineno, *(d.lineno for d in node.decorator_list)])
80	            body = lines[start - 1 : node.end_lineno]
81	            size = sum(is_code(line) for line in body)
82	            if size > limits["max_function_lines"]:
83	                problems.append(
84	                    f"{path}:{node.lineno}: SIZE003 function `{node.name}` has {size} code lines "
85	                    f"(max {limits['max_function_lines']})"
86	                )
87	    return problems
88	
89	
90	def main(argv: list[str]) -> int:
91	    limits = load_limits(Path("pyproject.toml"))
92	    problems: list[str] = []
93	    for f in iter_files(argv or ["src"]):
94	        problems += check_file(f, limits)
95	    if problems:
96	        print("\n".join(problems))
97	    return 1 if problems else 0
98	
99	
100	if __name__ == "__main__":
101	    sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))

1	# rllab — Tests
2	
3	**Package:** rllab · **Status:** Approved · **Last updated:** 2026-10-17 · **Pipelines:** [pipelines.md](pipelines.md)
4	
5	Board coordinates are `(row, col)` with row 0 at the top, so "up" decreases the row. Bodies are listed head first. Boards are set with `Snake.reset_to`. Snake and corridor cases address agent 0 of a batch of one (AD-09): an action list is one action per step, and an expected observation is agent 0's row.
6	
7	Tank positions are `(x, y)` in tank radii with the centre at the origin, set with `FishTank.reset_to`. The ball reflects when its edge reaches the wall, that is, when its centre is 0.92 from the origin. Predator-prey positions use the same frame, set with `PredatorPrey.reset_to`; each case's `input.json` lists all 6 prey; unless a case says otherwise, the other prey sit at `(−0.5, 0)`, `(0, 0.5)`, `(0, −0.5)`, `(−0.4, 0.4)`, `(−0.4, −0.4)` and the 8 pellets on the circle of radius 0.9 at 45° steps from `(0.9, 0)`, every animal at rest.

U06.S35Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-incremental-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ab96cc759c1057ea7/src/rllab/core/types.py (232 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-incremental-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ab96cc759c1057ea7/src/rllab/core/types.py
"""The shared types every component passes: teams, action spaces, steps and run settings.

Each team of an environment is batch-first over its agents (AD-09, AD-13). The dataclasses check
their contract invariants on construction, so an invalid value never exists.
"""

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any, Literal

import numpy.typing as npt

SOLO: str = "agents"
"""The one team name of every single-team environment (Snake, the corridor, the fish tank)."""

EnvName = Literal["snake", "corridor", "tank", "predprey"]
ViewName = Literal["compact", "grid", "index", "full", "limited"]
AgentName = Literal["tabular", "dqn", "reinforce", "ppo", "random", "scripted", "reference"]
OpponentKind = Literal["learn", "scripted", "random"]
SelfPlay = Literal["none", "latest", "pool"]
Ablation = Literal["none", "no_memory", "no_target", "no_explore"]

ENV_VIEWS: Mapping[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    "snake": ("compact", "grid"),
    "corridor": ("index",),
    "tank": ("full", "limited"),
    "predprey": ("limited",),
}
AGENTS: tuple[str, ...] = ("tabular", "dqn", "reinforce", "ppo", "random", "scripted", "reference")
OPPONENT_KINDS: tuple[str, ...] = ("learn", "scripted", "random")
SELF_PLAY: tuple[str, ...] = ("none", "latest", "pool")
ABLATION_AGENT: Mapping[str, str] = {"no_memory": "dqn", "no_target": "dqn", "no_explore": "tabular"}
PREDPREY_TEAMS = frozenset({"predator", "prey"})
MAX_SEED = 1_000_000
MAX_FISH = 16


def _check(ok: bool, key: str, message: str) -> None:
    """Raise `ValueError` naming `key` unless `ok`."""
    if not ok:
        raise ValueError(f"{key}: {message}")


def _check_choice(value: str, key: str, choices: tuple[str, ...]) -> None:
    _check(value in choices, key, f"{value!r} is not one of {', '.join(choices)}")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ActionSpace:
    """A team's actions: `n` discrete choices, or a `dim`-dimensional continuous vector."""

    kind: Literal["discrete", "continuous"]
    n: int = 0
    dim: int = 0

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        _check_choice(self.kind, "kind", ("discrete", "continuous"))
        if self.kind == "discrete":
            _check(self.n >= 2 and self.dim == 0, "n", "discrete needs n >= 2 and dim == 0")
        else:
            _check(self.dim >= 1 and self.n == 0, "dim", "continuous needs dim >= 1 and n == 0")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TeamSpec:
    """One team: its agent count, the per-agent observation shape and its action space."""

    n_agents: int
    obs_shape: tuple[int, ...]
    actions: ActionSpace

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        _check(self.n_agents >= 1, "n_agents", "a team has at least one agent")
        _check(all(d >= 1 for d in self.obs_shape), "obs_shape", "every dimension is >= 1")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class StepResult:
    """One environment step, every map keyed by team and batch-first over that team's agents."""

    obs: dict[str, npt.NDArray[Any]]
    reward: dict[str, npt.NDArray[Any]]
    done: dict[str, npt.NDArray[Any]]
    terminated: bool
    truncated: bool
    info: dict[str, float]

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        teams = self.obs.keys()
        _check(self.reward.keys() == teams == self.done.keys(), "obs", "one entry per team")
        _check(not (self.terminated and self.truncated), "truncated", "at most one game end")
        _check({"steps", "score"} <= self.info.keys(), "info", "holds steps and score")
        for team in teams:
            n = len(self.obs[team])
            _check(self.reward[team].shape == (n,), "reward", f"{team} is (n_agents,)")
            _check(self.done[team].shape == (n,), "done", f"{team} is (n_agents,)")
            _check(f"score:{team}" in self.info, "info", f"holds score:{team}")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Transition:
    """One learning team's step, every array batch-first over the team's agents."""

    obs: npt.NDArray[Any]
    actions: npt.NDArray[Any]
    reward: npt.NDArray[Any]
    next_obs: npt.NDArray[Any]
    terminated: npt.NDArray[Any]
    active: npt.NDArray[Any]
    truncated: bool

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        n = len(self.obs)
        arrays = (self.actions, self.reward, self.next_obs, self.terminated, self.active)
        _check(all(len(a) == n for a in arrays), "obs", "every array has one row per agent")
        _check(self.next_obs.shape == self.obs.shape, "next_obs", "has the shape of obs")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Rewards:
    """Reward sizes; the corridor's goal is `food`, the tank's survival per fish-step `step`."""

    food: float = 1.0
    death: float = -1.0
    step: float = 0.0


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Exploration:
    """A random-action rate falling linearly from `start` to `end` over `decay_steps`."""

    start: float = 1.0
    end: float = 0.05
    decay_steps: int = 50_000

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        _check(0.0 <= self.end <= self.start <= 1.0, "exploration", "needs 0 <= end <= start <= 1")
        _check(self.decay_steps >= 1, "decay_steps", "is at least 1")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AgentParams:
    """Learner hyperparameters; each learner reads only the fields its contract names."""

    lr: float
    gamma: float
    batch_size: int = 64
    memory_size: int = 50_000
    target_every: int = 1_000
    learn_start: int = 1_000
    hidden: int = 128
    net: Literal["mlp", "cnn"] = "mlp"
    rollout_steps: int = 2048
    epochs: int = 10
    clip: float = 0.2
    gae_lambda: float = 0.95
    games_per_update: int = 10

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        _check(self.lr > 0.0, "lr", "is positive")
        _check(0.0 < self.gamma <= 1.0, "gamma", "needs 0 < gamma <= 1")
        _check_choice(self.net, "net", ("mlp", "cnn"))


@dataclass(frozen=True, kw_only=True)
class RunSettings:
    """Everything one run needs (RUN-01): environment, view, agent, rewards, budget and seed."""

    name: str
    env: EnvName
    view: ViewName
    agent: AgentName
    n_agents: int = 1
    opponents: Mapping[str, OpponentKind] = field(default_factory=dict[str, OpponentKind])
    self_play: SelfPlay = "none"
    pool_latest: float = 0.5
    generations: int = 0
    ablation: Ablation = "none"
    rewards: Rewards = field(default_factory=Rewards)
    exploration: Exploration = field(default_factory=Exploration)
    params: AgentParams
    steps: int
    seed: int = 0
    eval_every: int
    eval_games: int

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        _check_choice(self.env, "env", tuple(ENV_VIEWS))
        _check_choice(self.view, "view", ENV_VIEWS[self.env])
        _check_choice(self.agent, "agent", AGENTS)
        _check_choice(self.ablation, "ablation", ("none", *ABLATION_AGENT))
        _check_choice(self.self_play, "self_play", SELF_PLAY)
        _check(0 <= self.seed < MAX_SEED, "seed", f"is in [0, {MAX_SEED})")
        _check(self.steps > 0, "steps", "is positive")
        _check(self.eval_every > 0, "eval_every", "is positive")
        _check(self.eval_games >= 1, "eval_games", "is at least 1")
        _check(0.0 <= self.pool_latest <= 1.0, "pool_latest", "is in [0, 1]")
        _check_agent(self)
        _check_teams(self)


def _check_agent(s: RunSettings) -> None:
    """The agent belongs to the environment, and the ablation to the agent."""
    if s.agent in ("tabular", "dqn"):
        _check(s.env in ("snake", "corridor"), "agent", f"{s.agent} needs snake or corridor")
    if s.agent in ("reinforce", "ppo"):
        _check(s.env in ("tank", "predprey"), "agent", f"{s.agent} needs the tank")
    if s.env == "predprey":
        _check(s.agent in ("ppo", "reference"), "agent", "predprey needs ppo or reference")
    if s.agent == "reference" and s.env == "tank":
        _check(s.n_agents == 1 and s.view == "full", "agent", "tank reference: 1 fish, full view")
    if s.ablation != "none":
        wanted = ABLATION_AGENT[s.ablation]
        _check(s.agent == wanted, "ablation", f"{s.ablation} needs agent {wanted}")
    top = MAX_FISH if s.env == "tank" else 1
    _check(1 <= s.n_agents <= top, "n_agents", f"is in [1, {top}] for {s.env}")


def _check_teams(s: RunSettings) -> None:
    """Opponents, self-play and generations: only predator-prey has a second team."""
    if s.env != "predprey":
        _check(not s.opponents, "opponents", "single-team runs have none")
        _check(s.self_play == "none", "self_play", "single-team runs use none")
        _check(s.generations == 0, "generations", "single-team runs save none")
        return
    _check(s.opponents.keys() == PREDPREY_TEAMS, "opponents", "names predator and prey")
    for kind in s.opponents.values():
        _check_choice(kind, "opponents", OPPONENT_KINDS)
    learners = [team for team, kind in s.opponents.items() if kind == "learn"]
    both = len(learners) == len(PREDPREY_TEAMS)
    _check((s.self_play != "none") == both, "self_play", "latest or pool only when both learn")
    _check(s.generations == (10 if learners else 0), "generations", "10 when a team learns")

"""The two protocols every environment and every player meets (AD-03, AD-09, AD-13)."""

from collections.abc import Mapping
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal, Protocol

import numpy.typing as npt

from rllab.core.types import StepResult, TeamSpec, Transition


class Env(Protocol):
    """An environment of one or more teams, each batch-first over its agents.

    Observations map each team to float32 `(n_agents, *obs_shape)`; actions map each team to
    `(n_agents,)` int64 (discrete) or `(n_agents, dim)` float32 (continuous). `info` holds
    `steps`, `score:<team>` per team and `score` for the first team. `frame()` is an int8 grid of
    cell codes when `board == "grid"` and `(K, 4)` float32 circles when `board == "tank"`.
    `step` raises `RuntimeError` after the game is over and `ValueError` for a missing team or a
    malformed action.
    """

    teams: Mapping[str, TeamSpec]
    board: Literal["grid", "tank"]

    def reset(self, seed: int) -> tuple[dict[str, npt.NDArray[Any]], dict[str, float]]: ...

    def step(self, actions: Mapping[str, npt.NDArray[Any]]) -> StepResult: ...

    def frame(self) -> npt.NDArray[Any]: ...


class Agent(Protocol):
    """A player for one team: one action per agent of the team's `(n_agents, *obs_shape)` batch.

    `explore=False` never samples or takes a random action (AGENT-07). Players that don't learn
    make `learn` a no-op. `save` then `load` gives the same `act` outputs.
    """

    def act(self, obs: npt.NDArray[Any], *, explore: bool) -> npt.NDArray[Any]: ...

    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> None: ...

    def save(self, path: Path) -> None: ...

    def load(self, path: Path) -> None: ...

"""Run settings from TOML (RUN-01, AD-06)."""

from pathlib import Path

from rllab.core.types import RunSettings


def load_settings(path: Path) -> RunSettings:
    """Read a TOML settings file into a fully resolved `RunSettings`.

    Unspecified fields take the dataclass defaults. Raises `ValueError` naming the key for an
    unknown environment, view, agent, ablation or an out-of-range value, and `FileNotFoundError`
    if the file is missing.
    """
    raise NotImplementedError("M1-T02")


def with_seed(settings: RunSettings, seed: int) -> RunSettings:
    """The same settings with only the training seed swapped."""
    raise NotImplementedError("M1-T02")

"""Snake on a square grid, one team (`SOLO`) of one agent (SNAKE-01 to SNAKE-09)."""

from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from typing import Any, Literal

import numpy.typing as npt

from rllab.core import Env, Rewards, StepResult, TeamSpec

Heading = Literal["up", "right", "down", "left"]


class Snake(Env):
    """Snake with the compact `(11,)` or the grid `(3, size, size)` view; actions 0 straight,
    1 turn left, 2 turn right. Cells are `(row, col)`, row 0 at the top, bodies head first."""

    teams: Mapping[str, TeamSpec]
    board: Literal["grid", "tank"] = "grid"

    def __init__(
        self, *, view: Literal["compact", "grid"], rewards: Rewards = Rewards(), size: int = 10
    ) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T13")

    def reset(self, seed: int) -> tuple[dict[str, npt.NDArray[Any]], dict[str, float]]:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T13")

    def reset_to(
        self, body: Sequence[tuple[int, int]], heading: Heading, food: tuple[int, int]
    ) -> tuple[dict[str, npt.NDArray[Any]], dict[str, float]]:
        """Start from a given board, score and step count 0; returns what `reset` does.

        Raises `ValueError` if the body is not contiguous, leaves the grid, or the food is on it.
        """
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T13")

    def step(self, actions: Mapping[str, npt.NDArray[Any]]) -> StepResult:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T13")

    def frame(self) -> npt.NDArray[Any]:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T13")

"""The 1-D corridor (CORE-03): one team (`SOLO`) of one agent, actions 0 left and 1 right."""

from collections.abc import Mapping
from typing import Any, Literal

import numpy.typing as npt

from rllab.core import Env, Rewards, StepResult, TeamSpec


class Corridor(Env):
    """A row of `length` cells: start at the left end, `rewards.food` at the right end, and a
    game cap of `max_steps`. The observation is the one-hot position; `frame()` is `1 x length`."""

    teams: Mapping[str, TeamSpec]
    board: Literal["grid", "tank"] = "grid"

    def __init__(self, *, length: int = 10, max_steps: int = 50, rewards: Rewards = Rewards()) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T14")

    def reset(self, seed: int) -> tuple[dict[str, npt.NDArray[Any]], dict[str, float]]:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T14")

    def step(self, actions: Mapping[str, npt.NDArray[Any]]) -> StepResult:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T14")

    def frame(self) -> npt.NDArray[Any]:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T14")

"""The 1-D corridor (CORE-03): one team (`SOLO`) of one agent, actions 0 left and 1 right."""

from collections.abc import Mapping
from typing import Any, Literal

import numpy.typing as npt

from rllab.core import Env, Rewards, StepResult, TeamSpec
from rllab.envs.types import DEFAULT_REWARDS


class Corridor(Env):
    """A row of `length` cells: start at the left end, `rewards.food` at the right end, and a
    game cap of `max_steps`. The observation is the one-hot position; `frame()` is `1 x length`."""

    teams: Mapping[str, TeamSpec]
    board: Literal["grid", "tank"] = "grid"

    def __init__(
        self, *, length: int = 10, max_steps: int = 50, rewards: Rewards = DEFAULT_REWARDS
    ) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T14")

    def reset(self, seed: int) -> tuple[dict[str, npt.NDArray[Any]], dict[str, float]]:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T14")

    def step(self, actions: Mapping[str, npt.NDArray[Any]]) -> StepResult:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T14")

    def frame(self) -> npt.NDArray[Any]:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T14")

"""Snake on a square grid, one team (`SOLO`) of one agent (SNAKE-01 to SNAKE-09)."""

from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from typing import Any, Literal

import numpy.typing as npt

from rllab.core import Env, Rewards, StepResult, TeamSpec
from rllab.envs.types import DEFAULT_REWARDS

Heading = Literal["up", "right", "down", "left"]


class Snake(Env):
    """Snake with the compact `(11,)` or the grid `(3, size, size)` view; actions 0 straight,
    1 turn left, 2 turn right. Cells are `(row, col)`, row 0 at the top, bodies head first."""

    teams: Mapping[str, TeamSpec]
    board: Literal["grid", "tank"] = "grid"

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        view: Literal["compact", "grid"],
        rewards: Rewards = DEFAULT_REWARDS,
        size: int = 10,
    ) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T13")

    def reset(self, seed: int) -> tuple[dict[str, npt.NDArray[Any]], dict[str, float]]:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T13")

    def reset_to(
        self, body: Sequence[tuple[int, int]], heading: Heading, food: tuple[int, int]
    ) -> tuple[dict[str, npt.NDArray[Any]], dict[str, float]]:
        """Start from a given board, score and step count 0; returns what `reset` does.

        Raises `ValueError` if the body is not contiguous, leaves the grid, or the food is on it.
        """
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T13")

    def step(self, actions: Mapping[str, npt.NDArray[Any]]) -> StepResult:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T13")

    def frame(self) -> npt.NDArray[Any]:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T13")

from typing import Any

import numpy.typing as npt

from rllab.core import Agent, Transition

    """Uniform over `n_actions` from a seeded generator, whatever `explore` says; never learns."""

    def save(self, path: Path) -> None:

"""The scripted Snake baseline (BASE-02) on the compact view."""

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import numpy.typing as npt

from rllab.core import Agent, Transition


class ScriptedPlayer(Agent):
    """Toward the food if that move is safe, else any safe move, else straight; ties go
    straight, then left, then right. Deterministic; never learns.

    `act` raises `ValueError` if an observation is not shape `(11,)` (`envs.COMPACT_FIELDS`).
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T17")

    def act(self, obs: npt.NDArray[Any], *, explore: bool) -> npt.NDArray[Any]:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T17")

    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T17")

    def save(self, path: Path) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T17")

    def load(self, path: Path) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T17")

"""The table-based value learner (AGENT-01, AGENT-02, ABL-03), pure NumPy (AD-02)."""

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import numpy.typing as npt

from rllab.core import Agent, AgentParams, Exploration, Transition


class TabularAgent(Agent):
    """One value per (observation bytes, action), starting at 0, for a batch of one agent.

    Each `learn` moves the value toward `reward + gamma * max next value`, with no bootstrap
    when `terminated`. `explore_enabled=False` is the `no_explore` ablation. Checkpoints are NPZ.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        n_actions: int,
        params: AgentParams,
        exploration: Exploration,
        *,
        seed: int,
        explore_enabled: bool = True,
    ) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T15")

    def act(self, obs: npt.NDArray[Any], *, explore: bool) -> npt.NDArray[Any]:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T15")

    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T15")

    def save(self, path: Path) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T15")

    def load(self, path: Path) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T15")

"""The neural value learner (AGENT-03 to AGENT-05, ABL-01, ABL-02), on PyTorch (AD-02, AD-05)."""

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal

import numpy.typing as npt

from rllab.core import Agent, AgentParams, Exploration, TeamSpec, Transition


class DQNAgent(Agent):
    """A value network over a memory of past steps with a target copy, for a batch of one agent.

    Its observation shape and action count come from `team`. `ablation="no_memory"` updates on
    the latest transition only; `"no_target"` takes targets from the online network. No update
    happens before `params.learn_start` transitions. Checkpoints are a PyTorch state dict.

    Raises `ValueError` if `team` has more than one agent or continuous actions, or if
    `params.net == "cnn"` and `team.obs_shape` is not 3-D.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        team: TeamSpec,
        params: AgentParams,
        exploration: Exploration,
        *,
        seed: int,
        ablation: Literal["none", "no_memory", "no_target"] = "none",
    ) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T05")

"""What a run folder holds: its curve points, recorded games and the record itself (AD-06)."""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal

import numpy.typing as npt

from rllab.core import RunSettings


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CurvePoint:
    """One greedy evaluation of `eval_games` games during training."""

    step: int
    games: int
    mean_score: float
    elapsed_s: float

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.step < 0 or self.games < 0 or self.elapsed_s < 0:
            raise ValueError("step, games and elapsed_s are never negative")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class GameRecording:
    """One game: the start frame plus one frame per step, as grid cells or tank circles (AD-12).

    `rewards` is the per-step total over all agents (length T-1); `scores` maps each team to its
    score at every frame (length T).
    """

    seed: int
    board: Literal["grid", "tank"]
    frames: npt.NDArray[Any]
    rewards: npt.NDArray[Any]
    scores: dict[str, npt.NDArray[Any]]

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        n = len(self.frames)
        if n < 1 or self.frames.ndim != 3:
            raise ValueError("frames: T x H x W cells or T x K x 4 circles, T >= 1")
        if self.board == "tank" and self.frames.shape[2] != 4:
            raise ValueError("frames: tank circles are (x, y, radius, kind)")
        if self.rewards.shape != (n - 1,):
            raise ValueError("rewards: one total per step, length T - 1")
        if not self.scores or any(s.shape != (n,) for s in self.scores.values()):
            raise ValueError("scores: one score per frame for each team")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RunRecord:
    """A run folder as read back: settings, curve, status, checkpoints, generations, games."""

    path: Path
    settings: RunSettings
    curve: tuple[CurvePoint, ...]
    status: Literal["running", "complete", "incomplete"]
    checkpoints: dict[str, Path]
    generations: dict[str, tuple[Path, ...]]
    games: tuple[GameRecording, ...]

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        steps = [p.step for p in self.curve]
        if any(b <= a for a, b in zip(steps, steps[1:], strict=False)):
            raise ValueError("curve: steps strictly increase")
        if self.status == "complete" and not self.checkpoints:
            raise ValueError("checkpoints: a complete run has one per learning team")

"""One folder per run (AD-06): `runs/<name>-s<seed>-<timestamp>/` holding `settings.json`,
`curve.csv`, checkpoints, `generations/<team>/<index>`, `games.npz` and `status`."""

from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal

from rllab.core import SOLO, Agent, RunSettings
from rllab.runs.types import CurvePoint, GameRecording, RunRecord

RUNS_ROOT = Path("runs")


class RunWriter:
    """Writes one run folder as the run goes; each curve point is flushed at once."""

    def __init__(self, path: Path, settings: RunSettings) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T03")

    def add_point(self, p: CurvePoint) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T03")

    def save_checkpoint(self, agent: Agent, *, team: str = SOLO) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T03")

    def save_generation(self, agent: Agent, *, team: str, index: int) -> Path:
        """Write `generations/<team>/<index>` and return its path, so a pool can load it."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T03")

    def save_games(self, games: Sequence[GameRecording]) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T03")

    def finish(self, status: Literal["complete", "incomplete"]) -> RunRecord:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T03")


def create_run(settings: RunSettings, *, root: Path = RUNS_ROOT) -> RunWriter:
    """Make a uniquely named run folder holding `settings.json` and `status=running`."""
    raise NotImplementedError("M1-T03")


def open_run(path: Path) -> RunRecord:
    """Read a run folder back.

    Raises `FileNotFoundError` naming the run if the folder or `settings.json` is missing, and
    `ValueError` naming the file if a recording is unreadable.
    """
    raise NotImplementedError("M1-T03")

"""Evaluation results and how to evaluate (AD-17)."""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

import numpy.typing as npt

from rllab.core import SOLO
from rllab.runs import GameRecording

MAX_EVAL_GAMES = 1000


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ScoreSet:
    """One player's held-out scores for one training seed, one per game, and its recordings."""

    player: str
    train_seed: int
    scores: npt.NDArray[Any]
    games: tuple[GameRecording, ...]

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.scores.ndim != 1 or len(self.scores) < 1:
            raise ValueError("scores: one score per evaluation game")
        if len(self.games) > len(self.scores):
            raise ValueError("games: at most one recording per game played")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EvalOptions:
    """The team to score, the game count and how many games to record."""

    team: str = SOLO
    n_games: int = 200
    record: int = 0

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not 1 <= self.n_games <= MAX_EVAL_GAMES:
            raise ValueError(f"n_games: is in [1, {MAX_EVAL_GAMES}]")
        if not 0 <= self.record <= self.n_games:
            raise ValueError("record: is in [0, n_games]")

"""Greedy evaluation on held-out seeds (EVAL-01, DATA-01, AGENT-07, QR-04)."""

from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping

from rllab.core import Agent, Env
from rllab.evaluate.types import EvalOptions, ScoreSet

DEFAULT_OPTIONS = EvalOptions()


def eval_seeds(train_seed: int, n: int) -> list[int]:
    """`1_000_000 + 1000 * train_seed + i` for `i < n`, disjoint from every training seed.

    Raises `ValueError` if `n` > 1000 or `train_seed` >= 1,000,000.
    """
    raise NotImplementedError("M1-T18")


def evaluate(
    make_env: Callable[[], Env],
    agents: Mapping[str, Agent],
    *,
    player: str,
    train_seed: int,
    options: EvalOptions = DEFAULT_OPTIONS,
) -> ScoreSet:
    """Play `options.n_games` games on `eval_seeds(train_seed, options.n_games)` with one agent
    per team and `explore=False`, never calling `learn`; score `options.team`'s `score:<team>`
    and record the first `options.record` games."""
    raise NotImplementedError("M1-T18")

"""The environment-agnostic training loop (CORE-02, RUN-02 to RUN-04)."""

from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.core import Agent, Env, RunSettings
from rllab.runs import RunRecord

RUNS_ROOT = Path("runs")


def train(
    make_env: Callable[[], Env],
    agents: Mapping[str, Agent],
    settings: RunSettings,
    *,
    root: Path = RUNS_ROOT,
) -> RunRecord:
    """Step the environment for `settings.steps` steps with one agent per team, calling each
    learning team's `learn` with its batch-first `Transition` after every step.

    Evaluates every `eval_every` steps (step 0 included) into a curve point, prints a progress
    line at least every 30 s, and saves each learner's checkpoint and 5 recorded games at the
    end. On `KeyboardInterrupt` it finishes the run `incomplete`, then re-raises.
    """
    raise NotImplementedError("M1-T06")

        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T08")

        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T08")

        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T08")

    def save(self, path: Path) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T08")

    def load(self, path: Path) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T08")

"""Claims to test and their tested results (EVAL-02 to EVAL-05, REF-02, REF-03)."""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Literal


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ClaimSpec:
    """"`a` greater than `b`" or "`a` not worse than `b`", optionally with REF-02's ratio bar
    `mean(a) >= min_ratio * mean(b)`. A stretch claim is reported but never a pass condition."""

    name: str
    a: str
    b: str
    direction: Literal["greater", "not_worse"]
    stretch: bool = False
    min_ratio: float | None = None

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.direction not in ("greater", "not_worse"):
            raise ValueError(f"direction: {self.direction!r} is not greater or not_worse")
        if self.a == self.b:
            raise ValueError("b: a claim compares two different players")
        if self.min_ratio is not None and self.min_ratio <= 0:
            raise ValueError("min_ratio: is positive")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Claim:
    """A tested claim: both means, raw and corrected p-values, the verdict and the method the
    `null-hypothesis-testing` skill chose (statistic, null distribution, correction)."""

    spec: ClaimSpec
    mean_a: float
    mean_b: float
    p_value: float
    p_corrected: float
    passed: bool
    method: str

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not (0.0 <= self.p_value <= 1.0 and 0.0 <= self.p_corrected <= 1.0):
            raise ValueError("p_value: p-values are in [0, 1]")
        if not self.method:
            raise ValueError("method: names the skill's choice")

    under a "Cross-play" section.

"""What a report names: its players, their roles on the charts, and the runs behind the curves."""

from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.core import SOLO
from rllab.runs import RunRecord

BASELINES = ("random", "scripted")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PlayerTarget:
    """One report player: its run folders (one per training seed) or a baseline name."""

    runs: tuple[Path, ...] = ()
    baseline: str | None = None
    team: str = SOLO
    vs: tuple[str, ...] = ()

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if bool(self.runs) == (self.baseline is not None):
            raise ValueError("runs: set exactly one of runs and baseline")
        if self.baseline is not None and self.baseline not in BASELINES:
            raise ValueError(f"baseline: {self.baseline!r} is not one of {', '.join(BASELINES)}")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ReportTargets:
    """The players and, in legend order, the baselines, ladder and ablation groups; `crossplay`
    holds the cross-play chart images to link."""

    players: Mapping[str, PlayerTarget]
    baselines: tuple[str, ...]
    ladder: tuple[str, ...]
    ablations: tuple[str, ...]
    crossplay: tuple[Path, ...] = ()

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        for key, names in (("baselines", self.baselines), ("ladder", self.ladder)):
            _check_named(key, names, self.players)
        _check_named("ablations", self.ablations, self.players)


def _check_named(key: str, names: Sequence[str], players: Mapping[str, PlayerTarget]) -> None:
    missing = [n for n in names if n not in players]
    if missing:
        raise ValueError(f"{key}: {', '.join(missing)} not among the players")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CurveRuns:
    """The ladder and ablation groups, one `RunRecord` per training seed, keyed by player."""

    ladder: Mapping[str, Sequence[RunRecord]]
    ablations: Mapping[str, Sequence[RunRecord]]

"""Players from run folders or baseline names, their scores, and the runs behind the curves."""

from rllab.core import Agent, RunSettings
from rllab.evaluate import ScoreSet
from rllab.lab.types import CurveRuns, ReportTargets


def load_player(
    target: str, seed: int, *, env: str, team: str, vs: str
) -> tuple[dict[str, Agent], RunSettings]:
    """One agent per team, with the settings to build the environment from.

    `team`'s agent comes from the run folder `target` (its settings, `make_env`, `make_agent`
    and that team's checkpoint) or is the baseline `target` names (`random`, `scripted`) on the
    default settings of `env`; every other team's comes from `vs`. Single-team environments
    ignore `team` and `vs`.
    """
    raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")

    raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")

    """The ladder and ablation groups' run records.

    Raises `ValueError` naming them if an ablation's seeds or `steps` differ from its full
    agent's.
    """
    raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-incremental-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ab96cc759c1057ea7/src/rllab/lab/cli.py
"""The command line: one entry call per seed or target.

    python -m rllab.lab train <settings.toml> [--seeds 0-4]
    python -m rllab.lab eval <run | random | scripted>... [--env E] [--team T] [--vs V] [--seed S]
    python -m rllab.lab report <report.toml>
    python -m rllab.lab replay <run> [--game N | --new --seed S | --pair <predator> <prey>]

`train` runs PL-02 when the settings name the `reference` agent, else PL-01. `--smoke` selects
Matplotlib's non-interactive backend, so a smoke run never opens a window.
"""

import argparse
import sys
from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

import matplotlib as mpl

from rllab.core import SOLO, load_settings
from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl01_train import run_train
from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl02_train_reference import run_train_reference
from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl03_evaluate import run_evaluate
from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl04_report import run_report
from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl05_replay import run_replay

BAD_INPUT = 2


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    """Run one subcommand; 0 on success, 2 on bad settings or a missing run."""
    args = _parser().parse_args(argv)
    if args.smoke:
        mpl.use("Agg")
    try:
        _dispatch(args)
    except (ValueError, FileNotFoundError) as exc:
        print(exc, file=sys.stderr)
        return BAD_INPUT
    return 0


def _dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    if args.command == "train":
        _train(args.settings, args.seeds)
    elif args.command == "eval":
        for target in args.targets:
            run_evaluate(target, args.seed, env=args.env, team=args.team, vs=args.vs)
    elif args.command == "report":
        run_report(args.spec, args.spec.with_suffix(""))
    else:
        new_seed = args.seed if args.new else None
        run_replay(args.run, args.game, new_seed, args.pair)


def _train(settings_path: Path, seeds: list[int] | None) -> None:
    settings = load_settings(settings_path)
    run = run_train_reference if settings.agent == "reference" else run_train
    for seed in seeds if seeds is not None else [settings.seed]:
        run(settings_path, seed)


def _seed_range(text: str) -> list[int]:
    """`3` or an inclusive range `0-4`."""
    first, _, last = text.partition("-")
    try:
        low, high = int(first), int(last or first)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError(f"seeds: {text!r} is not N or N-M") from exc
    if high < low:
        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError(f"seeds: {text!r} ends before it starts")
    return list(range(low, high + 1))


def _parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    common = argparse.ArgumentParser(add_help=False)
    common.add_argument("--smoke", action="store_true", help="headless: never open a window")
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="python -m rllab.lab", description=__doc__)
    commands = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)

    train = commands.add_parser("train", parents=[common], help="train runs (PL-01, PL-02)")
    train.add_argument("settings", type=Path)
    train.add_argument("--seeds", type=_seed_range, help="N or N-M; default: the settings' seed")

    evaluate = commands.add_parser("eval", parents=[common], help="evaluate players (PL-03)")
    evaluate.add_argument("targets", nargs="+", help="run folders, random or scripted")
    evaluate.add_argument("--env", choices=("snake", "tank", "predprey"), default="snake")
    evaluate.add_argument("--team", default=SOLO)
    evaluate.add_argument("--vs", default="scripted")
    evaluate.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)

    report = commands.add_parser("report", parents=[common], help="tested claims (PL-04)")
    report.add_argument("spec", type=Path)

    replay = commands.add_parser("replay", parents=[common], help="replay a game (PL-05)")
    replay.add_argument("run", type=Path)
    which = replay.add_mutually_exclusive_group()
    which.add_argument("--game", type=int, default=0)
    which.add_argument("--new", action="store_true", help="record a new game on --seed")
    which.add_argument("--pair", type=int, nargs=2, metavar=("PREDATOR", "PREY"))
    replay.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
    return parser

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-incremental-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ab96cc759c1057ea7/tests/contracts/codec.py
"""Contract samples: JSON files decoded strictly into the frozen types.

Each `tests/contracts/<component>.<Symbol>/samples/<case>.json` holds one case. The frozen type
is the schema: decoding rejects unknown and missing fields, wrong JSON types, values outside a
`Literal`, and then the type's own construction checks its contract invariants. Arrays are
`{"dtype": ..., "shape": [...], "data": [...]}`, paths are strings, tuples are lists.
"""

import dataclasses
import json
import types
from collections.abc import Mapping
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal, TypeVar, Union, get_args, get_origin, get_type_hints

import numpy as np

CONTRACTS = Path(__file__).parent
T = TypeVar("T")


def folder(symbol: str) -> Path:
    """The contract folder of `symbol`, such as `core.Env`."""
    return CONTRACTS / symbol


def cases(symbol: str) -> list[str]:
    """The case names of `symbol`'s samples, sorted."""
    return sorted(p.stem for p in (folder(symbol) / "samples").glob("*.json"))


def sample(symbol: str, case: str, tp: type[T]) -> T:
    """One sample of `symbol`, decoded into `tp`."""
    path = folder(symbol) / "samples" / f"{case}.json"
    value: T = decode(tp, json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")))
    return value


def decode(tp: Any, value: Any) -> Any:
    """`value` (parsed JSON) as an instance of the type `tp`; `ValueError` if it doesn't fit."""
    origin, args = get_origin(tp), get_args(tp)
    if tp is Any:
        return value
    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(tp):
        return _dataclass(tp, value)
    if origin is np.ndarray:
        return _array(value)
    if origin in (Union, types.UnionType):
        return _union(args, value)
    if origin is Literal:
        if value not in args:
            raise ValueError(f"{value!r} is not one of {args}")
        return value
    if origin is tuple:
        return _tuple(args, _expect(value, list))
    if origin in (dict, Mapping):
        return {k: decode(args[1], v) for k, v in _expect(value, dict).items()}
    return _scalar(tp, value)


def _dataclass(tp: Any, value: Any) -> Any:
    hints = get_type_hints(tp)
    fields = {f.name for f in dataclasses.fields(tp)}
    unknown = _expect(value, dict).keys() - fields
    if unknown:
        raise ValueError(f"{tp.__name__}: unknown fields {sorted(unknown)}")
    return tp(**{k: decode(hints[k], v) for k, v in value.items()})


def _array(value: Any) -> np.ndarray[Any, Any]:
    spec = _expect(value, dict)
    if spec.keys() != {"dtype", "shape", "data"}:
        raise ValueError(f"an array is dtype, shape and data, not {sorted(spec)}")
    return np.asarray(spec["data"], dtype=spec["dtype"]).reshape(spec["shape"])


def _union(args: tuple[Any, ...], value: Any) -> Any:
    if value is None and type(None) in args:
        return None
    (inner,) = [a for a in args if a is not type(None)]
    return decode(inner, value)


def _tuple(args: tuple[Any, ...], items: list[Any]) -> tuple[Any, ...]:
    if len(args) == 2 and args[1] is Ellipsis:
        return tuple(decode(args[0], v) for v in items)
    if len(args) != len(items):
        raise ValueError(f"expected {len(args)} items, got {len(items)}")
    return tuple(decode(a, v) for a, v in zip(args, items, strict=True))


def _scalar(tp: Any, value: Any) -> Any:
    if tp is Path:
        return Path(_expect(value, str))
    if tp is float and isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool):
        return float(value)
    if tp in (int, float, str, bool):
        if isinstance(value, bool) != (tp is bool):
            raise ValueError(f"{value!r} is not a {tp.__name__}")
        return _expect(value, tp)
    raise TypeError(f"no JSON decoding for {tp!r}")


def _expect(value: Any, tp: type[T]) -> T:
    if not isinstance(value, tp):
        raise ValueError(f"{value!r} is not a {tp.__name__}")
    return value

"""Contract samples: JSON files decoded strictly into the frozen types.

Each `tests/contracts/<component>.<Symbol>/samples/<case>.json` holds one case. The frozen type
is the schema: decoding rejects unknown fields, wrong JSON types and values outside a `Literal`,
and then the type's own construction checks its contract invariants (and missing fields).
Arrays are `{"dtype": ..., "shape": [...], "data": [...]}`, paths are strings, tuples are lists.
"""

import dataclasses
import json
import types
from collections.abc import Mapping
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal, Union, get_args, get_origin, get_type_hints

import numpy as np

CONTRACTS = Path(__file__).parent


def folder(symbol: str) -> Path:
    """The contract folder of `symbol`, such as `core.Env`."""
    return CONTRACTS / symbol


def cases(symbol: str) -> list[str]:
    """The case names of `symbol`'s samples, sorted."""
    return sorted(p.stem for p in (folder(symbol) / "samples").glob("*.json"))


def sample[T](symbol: str, case: str, tp: type[T]) -> T:
    """One sample of `symbol`, decoded into `tp`."""
    path = folder(symbol) / "samples" / f"{case}.json"
    value: T = decode(tp, json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")))
    return value


def decode(tp: Any, value: Any) -> Any:
    """`value` (parsed JSON) as an instance of the type `tp`; raises if it doesn't fit."""
    origin, args = get_origin(tp), get_args(tp)
    if tp is Any:
        decoded = value
    elif dataclasses.is_dataclass(tp):
        decoded = _dataclass(tp, value)
    elif origin is np.ndarray:
        decoded = _array(value)
    elif origin in (Union, types.UnionType):
        decoded = _union(args, value)
    elif origin is Literal:
        decoded = _literal(args, value)
    elif origin is tuple:
        decoded = _tuple(args, _expect(value, list))
    elif origin in (dict, Mapping):
        decoded = {k: decode(args[1], v) for k, v in _expect(value, dict).items()}
    else:
        decoded = _scalar(tp, value)
    return decoded


def _dataclass(tp: Any, value: Any) -> Any:
    hints = get_type_hints(tp)
    fields = {f.name for f in dataclasses.fields(tp)}
    unknown = _expect(value, dict).keys() - fields
    if unknown:
        raise ValueError(f"{tp.__name__}: unknown fields {sorted(unknown)}")
    return tp(**{k: decode(hints[k], v) for k, v in value.items()})


def _array(value: Any) -> np.ndarray[Any, Any]:
    spec = _expect(value, dict)
    if spec.keys() != {"dtype", "shape", "data"}:
        raise ValueError(f"an array is dtype, shape and data, not {sorted(spec)}")
    return np.asarray(spec["data"], dtype=spec["dtype"]).reshape(spec["shape"])


def _union(args: tuple[Any, ...], value: Any) -> Any:
    if value is None and type(None) in args:
        return None
    (inner,) = [a for a in args if a is not type(None)]
    return decode(inner, value)


def _literal(args: tuple[Any, ...], value: Any) -> Any:
    if value not in args:
        raise ValueError(f"{value!r} is not one of {args}")
    return value


def _tuple(args: tuple[Any, ...], items: list[Any]) -> tuple[Any, ...]:
    if len(args) == 2 and args[1] is Ellipsis:
        return tuple(decode(args[0], v) for v in items)
    if len(args) != len(items):
        raise ValueError(f"expected {len(args)} items, got {len(items)}")
    return tuple(decode(a, v) for a, v in zip(args, items, strict=True))


def _scalar(tp: Any, value: Any) -> Any:
    if tp is Path:
        return Path(_expect(value, str))
    if tp is float and isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool):
        return float(value)
    if tp not in (int, float, str, bool):
        raise TypeError(f"no JSON decoding for {tp!r}")
    if isinstance(value, bool) != (tp is bool):
        raise TypeError(f"{value!r} is not a {tp.__name__}")
    return _expect(value, tp)


def _expect[T](value: Any, tp: type[T]) -> T:
    if not isinstance(value, tp):
        raise TypeError(f"{value!r} is not a {tp.__name__}")
    return value
